<script>
(() => {
'use strict';
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const scene=$('scene'),deskC=$('desk'),motesC=$('motes'),paperC=$('paper');
const rear=$('rear'),carBack=$('carBack'),carFront=$('carFront'),bodyS=$('body'),guide=$('guide'),frontClip=$('frontClip'),sheet=$('sheet'),curl=$('curl'),shadow=$('shadow');
const reduceMQ=window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
let reduced=reduceMQ.matches;
const MONO='"Courier Prime","American Typewriter","Courier New","Nimbus Mono PS",Courier,monospace';
const STORE='marlowe44.letter.v1';

/* ---------- paper geometry, in paper units (600 × 776, drawn at 2×) ---------- */
const PW=600,PH=776,ML=60,TM=76,CW=10,LH=26,COLS=48,LINES=26,FS=16.6;
function mulberry32(a){return function(){a|=0;a=a+0x6D2B79F5|0;let t=Math.imul(a^a>>>15,1|a);t=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,61|t)^t;return((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296;};}

/* ---------- state ---------- */
let col=0,line=0,red=false,sheetNo=1,bellRung=false,shiftOn=false,lockOn=false;
let strokes=[]; // [ch,col,line,red,seed]
let muted=false;

/* ---------- paper canvas ---------- */
const P=paperC.getContext('2d');
const glyph=document.createElement('canvas');glyph.width=glyph.height=72;const G=glyph.getContext('2d');
const weave=document.createElement('canvas');weave.width=weave.height=144;
(function(){const w=weave.getContext('2d');const r=mulberry32(4);
  for(let i=0;i<1700;i++){w.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,'+(0.25+r()*0.75)+')';const x=r()*144,y=r()*144;w.fillRect(x,y,1+r()*1.6,1+r()*1.2);}
  w.globalAlpha=.35;w.strokeStyle='#000';w.lineWidth=.7;
  for(let i=-144;i<288;i+=4){w.beginPath();w.moveTo(i,0);w.lineTo(i+144,144);w.stroke();}
})();
function paintPaper(seed){
  const r=mulberry32(seed*7919+13);
  P.setTransform(2,0,0,2,0,0);P.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';P.globalAlpha=1;
  P.fillStyle='#faf6ec';P.fillRect(0,0,PW,PH);
  // cloudy formation of the sheet
  for(let i=0;i<60;i++){const x=r()*PW,y=r()*PH,rad=30+r()*90;const g=P.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,rad);const d=r()<.5;g.addColorStop(0,d?'rgba(214,200,170,0.07)':'rgba(255,255,250,0.1)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');P.fillStyle=g;P.fillRect(x-rad,y-rad,rad*2,rad*2);}
  // fibres
  P.lineCap='round';
  for(let i=0;i<900;i++){
    const x=r()*PW,y=r()*PH,a=r()*Math.PI,l=2+r()*9;
    P.strokeStyle=r()<.7?'rgba(160,140,105,'+(0.05+r()*0.08)+')':'rgba(120,135,160,'+(0.04+r()*0.05)+')';
    P.lineWidth=.3+r()*.4;P.beginPath();P.moveTo(x,y);P.quadraticCurveTo(x+Math.cos(a+.6)*l*.5,y+Math.sin(a+.6)*l*.5,x+Math.cos(a)*l,y+Math.sin(a)*l);P.stroke();
  }
  for(let i=0;i<5200;i++){P.fillStyle=r()<.5?'rgba(120,100,70,0.06)':'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';P.fillRect(r()*PW,r()*PH,.5+r()*.6,.5+r()*.6);}
  // watermark (fictional paper maker)
  P.save();P.translate(PW/2,PH*0.62);P.globalAlpha=.045;P.fillStyle='#7a6a4a';P.textAlign='center';
  P.font='italic 30px Georgia,"Times New Roman",serif';P.fillText('Marlowe Bond',0,0);
  P.font='11px '+MONO;P.fillText('25% COTTON FIBRE · No. 44',0,22);
  P.strokeStyle='#7a6a4a';P.lineWidth=1;P.beginPath();P.ellipse(0,-8,122,40,0,0,Math.PI*2);P.stroke();
  P.restore();
  // edge darkening + deckle shading
  const eg=P.createLinearGradient(0,0,PW,0);eg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(150,125,85,0.1)');eg.addColorStop(.04,'rgba(150,125,85,0)');eg.addColorStop(.96,'rgba(150,125,85,0)');eg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(150,125,85,0.1)');
  P.fillStyle=eg;P.fillRect(0,0,PW,PH);
  const tg=P.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,PH);tg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(150,125,85,0.1)');tg.addColorStop(.03,'rgba(150,125,85,0)');tg.addColorStop(.97,'rgba(150,125,85,0)');tg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(150,125,85,0.12)');
  P.fillStyle=tg;P.fillRect(0,0,PW,PH);
}
const inkRGB=[[35,31,28],[176,36,28]];
function stampOne(s){
  const [ch,c,l,isRed,seed]=s;
  if(ch===' ')return;
  const r=mulberry32(seed);
  const rgb=inkRGB[isRed?1:0];
  let dens=0.8+r()*0.2;
  if(r()<0.07)dens*=0.58;                                  // a timid keystroke
  dens*=0.9+0.1*Math.sin(c*0.31+l*1.7+seed%7);             // ribbon wear along the line
  if(/[A-Z0-9#%&@$]/.test(ch))dens=Math.min(1,dens*1.06);   // heavier letters strike harder
  const x=ML+c*CW+(r()-0.5)*0.9;
  const y=TM+l*LH+(r()-0.5)*1.7;
  // glyph on its own little plate (units ×2)
  G.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);G.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';G.globalAlpha=1;G.clearRect(0,0,72,72);
  G.translate(36,48);G.rotate((r()-0.5)*0.045);
  G.font='700 '+(FS*2)+'px '+MONO;G.textAlign='center';G.textBaseline='alphabetic';
  G.shadowColor='rgba('+rgb[0]+','+rgb[1]+','+rgb[2]+',0.55)';G.shadowBlur=1.6;
  G.fillStyle='rgb('+rgb[0]+','+rgb[1]+','+rgb[2]+')';
  G.fillText(ch,0,0);
  G.shadowBlur=0;G.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
  // fabric ribbon: knock out specks of ink
  G.globalCompositeOperation='destination-out';
  G.globalAlpha=0.3+(1-dens)*0.9;G.drawImage(weave,-r()*72,-r()*72);
  // uneven platen contact: one half of the letter prints lighter
  if(r()<0.4){const top=r()<.5;const g=G.createLinearGradient(0,top?20:52,0,top?52:20);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,'+(0.25+r()*0.35)+')');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');G.globalAlpha=1;G.fillStyle=g;G.fillRect(0,0,72,72);}
  G.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';G.globalAlpha=1;
  // onto the paper
  P.setTransform(2,0,0,2,0,0);P.globalCompositeOperation='multiply';
  P.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,dens);P.drawImage(glyph,x+CW/2-18,y-24,36,36);
  if(r()<0.06){P.globalAlpha=dens*0.3;P.drawImage(glyph,x+CW/2-18+0.9,y-24+0.35,36,36);} // faint double strike
  P.globalAlpha=1;P.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
}
function redrawAll(){paintPaper(sheetNo);for(const s of strokes)stampOne(s);}

/* ---------- layout ---------- */
let SW=0,SH=0,MW=0,U=1,MX=0,MY=0,S=1,mobile=false,K=1,X0=0,PRINTY=0;
function layout(){
  const vv=window.visualViewport;
  SW=scene.clientWidth;SH=Math.round(vv&&mobile?Math.min(scene.clientHeight,vv.height):scene.clientHeight);
  mobile=SW<700;
  const hFit=(SH*(mobile?0.36:0.46))/0.45;
  MW=Math.round(mobile?Math.min(SW-8,hFit):Math.min(920,SW*0.64,hFit));
  U=MW/1000;
  MX=Math.round((SW-MW)/2);
  MY=Math.round(SH-450*U-(mobile?6:10));
  const paperFrac=mobile?0.86:0.6;
  S=paperFrac*MW/PW;
  const travel=COLS*CW*S;
  K=mobile?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(SW-PW*S-16)/travel)):1;
  X0=SW/2-(1-K)*travel/2;
  PRINTY=MY+80*U;
  const place=(el,x,y,w,h)=>{el.style.left=x+'px';el.style.top=y+'px';el.style.width=w+'px';if(h!==undefined)el.style.height=h+'px';};
  place(carBack,MX,MY,MW,120*U);place(carFront,MX,MY,MW,120*U);
  place(bodyS,MX,MY+100*U,MW,350*U);
  place(guide,MX,MY,MW,450*U);
  place(shadow,MX-MW*0.06,MY+300*U,MW*1.12,210*U);
  frontClip.style.left='0px';frontClip.style.top='0px';frontClip.style.width=SW+'px';frontClip.style.height=Math.round(MY+104*U)+'px';
  sheet.style.width=PW*S+'px';
  paperC.style.width=PW*S+'px';paperC.style.height=PH*S+'px';
  curl.style.top=(PRINTY-8*U)+'px';curl.style.height=(34*U)+'px';
  rear.style.width=PW*S+'px';
  buildScale();
  paintDesk();sizeMotes();
  placeProps();
  update(true);
}
function paperLeftFor(c){const px=X0+(1-K)*c*CW*S;return px-(ML+c*CW)*S;}
function update(instant){
  const els=[sheet,rear,carBack,carFront];
  if(instant)els.forEach(e=>e.classList.add('instant'));
  const cc=Math.min(col,COLS);
  const left=paperLeftFor(cc);
  const mid=SW/2-(PW/2)*S; // paper centred over the machine
  const shift=left-mid;
  sheet.style.left='0px';sheet.style.transform='translateX('+left+'px)';
  rear.style.left=left+'px';
  const carShift=shift+(SW/2-(MX+MW/2));
  carBack.style.transform=carFront.style.transform='translateX('+carShift+'px)';
  const baseY=TM+line*LH;
  paperC.style.transform='translateY('+(PRINTY-baseY*S)+'px)';
  const unfed=Math.max(0,PH-baseY-34);
  rear.style.height=(unfed*S*0.94)+'px';
  rear.style.top=(MY+62*U-unfed*S*0.94)+'px';
  // printing point (fixed on desktop, travels a little on phones)
  const px=X0+(1-K)*cc*CW*S;
  $('guidePt').setAttribute('transform','translate('+((px-MX)/U).toFixed(1)+' 0)');
  $('typebar').setAttribute('x2',((px-MX)/U).toFixed(1));
  if(instant){void sheet.offsetWidth;els.forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('instant'));}
  status();
}
function buildScale(){
  const g=$('scaleTicks');g.innerHTML='';
  const ns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  const rel=S/U;           // paper units → carriage units
  const mid=500-(PW/2)*rel;
  for(let c=0;c<=COLS;c++){
    const x=mid+(ML+c*CW+CW/2)*rel;
    const t=document.createElementNS(ns,'path');
    const big=c%5===0;t.setAttribute('d','M'+x.toFixed(1)+' 90v'+(big?4.5:2.6));t.setAttribute('stroke','#3b3634');t.setAttribute('stroke-width',big?1.1:.7);g.appendChild(t);
    if(big){const tx=document.createElementNS(ns,'text');tx.setAttribute('x',x.toFixed(1));tx.setAttribute('y','99.4');tx.setAttribute('text-anchor','middle');tx.setAttribute('font-size','5.2');tx.setAttribute('font-family','monospace');tx.setAttribute('fill','#2b2725');tx.textContent=c;g.appendChild(tx);}
  }
  const bell=mid+(ML+(COLS-6)*CW+CW/2)*rel;
  const m=document.createElementNS(ns,'path');m.setAttribute('d','M'+bell.toFixed(1)+' 94l2.6 -4.2h-5.2z');m.setAttribute('fill','#b3261e');g.appendChild(m);
}

/* ---------- the desk: procedural walnut + lamplight ---------- */
function paintDesk(){
  const dpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
  deskC.width=Math.round(SW*dpr);deskC.height=Math.round(scene.clientHeight*dpr);
  const d=deskC.getContext('2d');d.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  const w=SW,h=scene.clientHeight;const r=mulberry32(21);
  let y=-20,i=0;
  const tones=[[92,52,26],[104,60,31],[86,48,24],[98,56,28]];
  while(y<h){
    const ph=190+r()*80;const t=tones[i%4];
    const g=d.createLinearGradient(0,y,0,y+ph);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgb('+(t[0]+6)+','+(t[1]+4)+','+(t[2]+2)+')');g.addColorStop(1,'rgb('+(t[0]-8)+','+(t[1]-6)+','+(t[2]-4)+')');
    d.fillStyle=g;d.fillRect(0,y,w,ph);
    const n=Math.round(ph/2.4);
    for(let k=0;k<n;k++){
      const gy=y+r()*ph,amp=1+r()*5,f=0.0016+r()*0.005,p0=r()*6.28,dark=r()<.62;
      d.strokeStyle=dark?'rgba(38,18,6,'+(0.08+r()*0.2)+')':'rgba(176,112,60,'+(0.05+r()*0.12)+')';
      d.lineWidth=0.5+r()*1.7;d.beginPath();
      for(let x=0;x<=w+12;x+=12){const yy=gy+Math.sin(x*f+p0)*amp+Math.sin(x*f*3.3+p0*2)*amp*0.35;x?d.lineTo(x,yy):d.moveTo(x,yy);}
      d.stroke();
    }
    // a knot now and then
    if(r()<.55){const kx=r()*w,ky=y+ph*(.3+r()*.4);for(let q=10;q>0;q--){d.strokeStyle='rgba(40,18,6,'+(0.05+q*0.012)+')';d.lineWidth=1;d.beginPath();d.ellipse(kx,ky,q*7,q*2.2,0,0,Math.PI*2);d.stroke();}}
    d.fillStyle='rgba(18,8,2,.6)';d.fillRect(0,y+ph-2,w,2);
    d.fillStyle='rgba(255,196,130,.08)';d.fillRect(0,y+ph,w,1);
    y+=ph;i++;
  }
  // lamplight from the upper left + warm bloom behind the machine
  const L=d.createRadialGradient(w*0.18,-h*0.12,0,w*0.18,-h*0.12,Math.hypot(w,h)*0.95);
  L.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,214,150,0.5)');L.addColorStop(.35,'rgba(255,190,120,0.2)');L.addColorStop(.75,'rgba(40,18,6,0.15)');L.addColorStop(1,'rgba(10,4,1,0.62)');
  d.fillStyle=L;d.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
  const B=d.createRadialGradient(w/2,h*0.62,0,w/2,h*0.62,Math.max(w,h)*0.5);
  B.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,200,130,0.14)');B.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,200,130,0)');
  d.fillStyle=B;d.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
  // coffee ring, long dried
  if(!mobile){d.strokeStyle='rgba(60,28,8,0.22)';d.lineWidth=3;d.beginPath();d.arc(w*0.9,h*0.3,44,0.3,5.9);d.stroke();d.lineWidth=1;d.strokeStyle='rgba(60,28,8,0.14)';d.beginPath();d.arc(w*0.9+2,h*0.3+1,40,0,6.28);d.stroke();}
}
function placeProps(){
  const card=$('card'),tag=$('tag'),pencil=$('pencil');
  if(mobile){
    const hb=document.querySelector('header.top').getBoundingClientRect().bottom;
    card.style.left='18px';card.style.top=(hb+22)+'px';return;
  }
  card.style.left=Math.max(24,MX*0.5-140)+'px';card.style.top=(SH*0.44)+'px';
  pencil.style.left=Math.max(20,MX*0.5-110)+'px';pencil.style.top=(SH*0.44+236)+'px';
  tag.style.left=(MX+MW+Math.max(18,(SW-MX-MW)*0.5-140))+'px';tag.style.top=(MY+175*U)+'px';
}

/* ---------- dust motes in the lamp beam ---------- */
const M=motesC.getContext('2d');const motes=[];let mdpr=1;
function sizeMotes(){mdpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);motesC.width=Math.round(SW*mdpr);motesC.height=Math.round(scene.clientHeight*mdpr);
  if(!motes.length){const r=mulberry32(9);for(let i=0;i<46;i++)motes.push({x:r(),y:r(),z:.4+r()*.9,p:r()*6.28,s:.3+r()*.7});}}
function drawMotes(t){
  const w=SW,h=scene.clientHeight;M.setTransform(mdpr,0,0,mdpr,0,0);M.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
  for(const m of motes){
    if(!reduced){m.y-=0.00004*m.s*16;m.x+=Math.sin(t*0.0003+m.p)*0.00008;if(m.y<-0.05){m.y=1.05;m.x=Math.random();}}
    const x=m.x*w,y=m.y*h;
    const beam=Math.max(0,1-Math.hypot(x/w-0.2,y/h-0.05)*1.35);
    if(beam<=0)continue;
    const a=beam*(0.35+0.35*Math.sin(t*0.002+m.p*3));
    M.fillStyle='rgba(255,226,170,'+a.toFixed(3)+')';M.beginPath();M.arc(x,y,m.z*1.3,0,6.283);M.fill();
  }
}
let raf=0;function loop(t){raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);drawMotes(t);}
function startLoop(){if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);}
function stopLoop(){if(raf){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;}}
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>document.hidden?stopLoop():startLoop());

/* ---------- keyboard (built at runtime from the Marlowe layout) ---------- */
const SHIFTED={'1':'!','2':'"','3':'#','4':'$','5':'%','6':'_','7':'&','8':"'",'9':'(','0':')','-':'*',';':':',',':',','.':'.','/':'?'};
const ROWS=[
  {y:150,r:16.5,sp:58,dx:10,keys:['1','2','3','4','5','6','7','8','9','0','-','BS']},
  {y:190,r:17.5,sp:61,dx:6,keys:['TAB','q','w','e','r','t','y','u','i','o','p']},
  {y:230,r:18.5,sp:64,dx:3,keys:['LOCK','a','s','d','f','g','h','j','k','l',';']},
  {y:270,r:19.5,sp:67,dx:0,keys:['SHIFT','z','x','c','v','b','n','m',',','.','/','SHIFT']}
];
const keyEls=new Map();
function buildKeys(){
  const ns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg',host=$('keys');
  const mk=(t,a)=>{const e=document.createElementNS(ns,t);for(const k in a)e.setAttribute(k,a[k]);return e;};
  ROWS.forEach((row,ri)=>{
    const n=row.keys.length,x0=500+row.dx-(n-1)*row.sp/2;
    row.keys.forEach((k,i)=>{
      const x=x0+i*row.sp,y=row.y,r=row.r,ry=r*0.8;
      const outer=mk('g',{transform:'translate('+x+' '+y+')'});
      const g=mk('g',{class:'key','data-k':k,role:'button','aria-label':k==='BS'?'Back space':k==='TAB'?'Tab':k==='LOCK'?'Shift lock':k==='SHIFT'?'Shift':'Key '+k.toUpperCase()});
      g.appendChild(mk('rect',{x:-2.6,y:0,width:5.2,height:26+ri*2,fill:'#34302d'}));
      const kc=mk('g',{class:'kc'});
      kc.appendChild(mk('ellipse',{cx:0,cy:3,rx:r+3.5,ry:ry+3.5,fill:'rgba(20,30,25,.35)'}));
      kc.appendChild(mk('ellipse',{cx:0,cy:0,rx:r+3,ry:ry+3,fill:'url(#rim)'}));
      kc.appendChild(mk('ellipse',{cx:0,cy:-.6,rx:r,ry:ry,fill:'url(#cap)',class:'cap'}));
      const long=k.length>1;
      const label=k==='BS'?'⌫':(long?k:k.toUpperCase());
      const tx=mk('text',{x:0,y:long?2.6:r*0.34,'text-anchor':'middle','font-family':MONO,'font-weight':'700','font-size':long?(k==='SHIFT'?6.6:7.4):r*0.95,fill:'#2b2724'});
      tx.textContent=label;kc.appendChild(tx);
      if(SHIFTED[k]&&!/[a-z]/.test(k)&&SHIFTED[k]!==k){const st=mk('text',{x:r*0.52,y:-r*0.22,'text-anchor':'middle','font-family':MONO,'font-size':r*0.5,fill:'#8a2a22'});st.textContent=SHIFTED[k];kc.appendChild(st);}
      g.appendChild(kc);outer.appendChild(g);host.appendChild(outer);
      if(!keyEls.has(k))keyEls.set(k,[]);keyEls.get(k).push(g);
    });
  });
  keyEls.set(' ',[$('spaceKey')]);
}
function flashKey(k){
  const list=keyEls.get(k);if(!list)return;
  for(const g of list){g.classList.add('down');setTimeout(()=>g.classList.remove('down'),reduced?40:110);}
}
function keyFor(ch){
  if(/[a-z]/.test(ch))return ch;
  if(/[A-Z]/.test(ch))return ch.toLowerCase();
  if(ch===' ')return ' ';
  for(const k in SHIFTED){if(SHIFTED[k]===ch)return k;}
  return keyEls.has(ch)?ch:null;
}

/* ---------- sound (Web Audio, synthesized) ---------- */
let AC=null,master=null,noise=null;
function audio(){
  if(AC){if(AC.state==='suspended')AC.resume();return;}
  try{
    AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
    master=AC.createGain();master.gain.value=muted?0:0.6;master.connect(AC.destination);
    noise=AC.createBuffer(1,AC.sampleRate*0.6|0,AC.sampleRate);const d=noise.getChannelData(0);for(let i=0;i<d.length;i++)d[i]=Math.random()*2-1;
  }catch(e){AC=null;}
}
function burst(t,dur,type,freq,q,gain){
  const s=AC.createBufferSource();s.buffer=noise;const f=AC.createBiquadFilter();f.type=type;f.frequency.value=freq;f.Q.value=q;
  const g=AC.createGain();g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(gain,t+0.002);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+dur);
  s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(master);s.start(t,Math.random()*0.5);s.stop(t+dur+0.03);
}
function thump(t,f0,f1,dur,gain){
  const o=AC.createOscillator();o.type='triangle';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0,t);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f1,t+dur);
  const g=AC.createGain();g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(gain,t+0.003);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+dur);
  o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start(t);o.stop(t+dur+0.03);
}
const snd={
  key(){if(!AC||muted)return;const t=AC.currentTime;burst(t,0.035,'bandpass',2100+Math.random()*1500,1.1,0.9);thump(t,210,85,0.05,0.45);burst(t+0.024,0.018,'highpass',5400,0.7,0.22);},
  space(){if(!AC||muted)return;const t=AC.currentTime;burst(t,0.05,'bandpass',850,0.8,0.45);thump(t,150,60,0.07,0.5);burst(t+0.03,0.02,'highpass',4800,0.7,0.2);},
  soft(){if(!AC||muted)return;const t=AC.currentTime;burst(t,0.03,'bandpass',1400,1,0.35);},
  bell(){if(!AC||muted)return;const t=AC.currentTime;
    [[2093,0.32,1.9],[2093*2.76,0.1,0.8],[2093*5.4,0.05,0.35],[2093*1.5,0.04,0.6]].forEach(([f,gv,d])=>{const o=AC.createOscillator();o.type='sine';o.frequency.value=f;const g=AC.createGain();g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(gv,t+0.004);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+d);o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start(t);o.stop(t+d+0.05);});
    burst(t,0.02,'highpass',6000,0.7,0.2);},
  ret(){if(!AC||muted)return;const t=AC.currentTime;
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++)burst(t+i*0.028,0.016,'highpass',3600,0.8,0.3);
    const s=AC.createBufferSource();s.buffer=noise;const f=AC.createBiquadFilter();f.type='bandpass';f.Q.value=0.9;f.frequency.setValueAtTime(1400,t+0.1);f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(380,t+0.45);
    const g=AC.createGain();g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t+0.1);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.16,t+0.16);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+0.46);
    s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(master);s.start(t+0.1);s.stop(t+0.5);
    thump(t+0.45,130,48,0.1,0.75);burst(t+0.45,0.05,'bandpass',1500,1,0.55);},
  paper(){if(!AC||muted)return;const t=AC.currentTime;
    const s=AC.createBufferSource();s.buffer=noise;s.loop=true;const f=AC.createBiquadFilter();f.type='bandpass';f.Q.value=0.6;f.frequency.setValueAtTime(900,t);f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(3800,t+0.55);
    const g=AC.createGain();g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.28,t+0.08);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+0.7);
    s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(master);s.start(t);s.stop(t+0.75);
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++)burst(t+0.05+i*0.05,0.012,'highpass',4200,0.8,0.12);}
};

/* ---------- typing ---------- */
let seedCounter=1;
function strike(){
  const tb=$('typebar'),sp=$('spark');
  tb.classList.remove('strike');sp.classList.remove('strike');void tb.getBBox;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{tb.classList.add('strike');sp.classList.add('strike');});
}
function typeChar(ch){
  audio();
  if(line>=LINES){toast('The sheet is full — press <b>New sheet</b> for a fresh page');snd.soft();return;}
  const c=Math.min(col,COLS);
  const s=[ch,c,line,red?1:0,(Date.now()+seedCounter++*7919)%2147483647];
  strokes.push(s);stampOne(s);
  flashKey(keyFor(ch));strike();snd.key();
  if(col<COLS)col++;
  else toast('Margin locked — pull the lever or press <b>Return</b>');
  checkBell();update(false);persist();
  if(shiftOn&&!lockOn){shiftOn=false;syncShift();}
}
function space(){audio();flashKey(' ');snd.space();if(col<COLS)col++;checkBell();update(false);persist();}
function backspace(){audio();flashKey('BS');snd.soft();if(col>0)col--;update(false);persist();}
function tab(){audio();flashKey('TAB');snd.space();col=Math.min(COLS,(Math.floor(col/5)+1)*5);checkBell();update(false);persist();}
function carriageReturn(){
  audio();
  const lv=$('lever');lv.classList.remove('pull');void lv.getBBox;requestAnimationFrame(()=>lv.classList.add('pull'));
  snd.ret();
  [sheet,rear,carBack,carFront].forEach(e=>e.classList.add('returning'));
  setTimeout(()=>[sheet,rear,carBack,carFront].forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('returning')),520);
  col=0;bellRung=false;
  if(line<LINES)line++;
  if(line>=LINES)toast('Last line reached — the sheet is full');
  update(false);persist();
  announce('New line, line '+(line+1));
}
function checkBell(){
  if(!bellRung&&col>=COLS-6){bellRung=true;snd.bell();ding();}
}
function ding(){
  const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='ding';d.textContent='ding';
  const px=X0+(1-K)*Math.min(col,COLS)*CW*S;
  d.style.left=(px+40*U)+'px';d.style.top=(MY+20*U)+'px';scene.appendChild(d);setTimeout(()=>d.remove(),1400);
}

/* ---------- ribbon ---------- */
function setRibbon(isRed){
  red=isRed;$('inkBlack').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!red));$('inkRed').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(red));
  $('vibrator').setAttribute('transform',red?'translate(0 -3.8)':'translate(0 0)');
  audio();snd.soft();persist();
}
$('inkBlack').addEventListener('click',()=>setRibbon(false));
$('inkRed').addEventListener('click',()=>setRibbon(true));

/* ---------- shift ---------- */
function syncShift(){
  (keyEls.get('SHIFT')||[]).forEach(g=>g.classList.toggle('on',shiftOn&&!lockOn));
  (keyEls.get('LOCK')||[]).forEach(g=>g.classList.toggle('on',lockOn));
}
function onScreenKey(k){
  if(k==='BS'){backspace();return;}
  if(k==='TAB'){tab();return;}
  if(k==='SHIFT'){shiftOn=!shiftOn;audio();snd.soft();flashKey('SHIFT');syncShift();return;}
  if(k==='LOCK'){lockOn=!lockOn;shiftOn=lockOn;audio();snd.soft();syncShift();return;}
  if(k===' '){space();return;}
  const up=shiftOn||lockOn;
  let ch=k;
  if(/[a-z]/.test(k))ch=up?k.toUpperCase():k;
  else if(up&&SHIFTED[k])ch=SHIFTED[k];
  typeChar(ch);
}

/* ---------- input: physical keyboard, on-screen keys, phone keyboard ---------- */
const ghost=$('ghost');
window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey||e.altKey)return;
  const tgt=e.target,tag=tgt&&tgt.tagName;
  const onButton=tag==='BUTTON'||(tgt&&tgt.getAttribute&&tgt.getAttribute('role')==='button');
  if(e.key==='Enter'){
    if(onButton&&tgt.id!=='lever')return;
    e.preventDefault();carriageReturn();return;
  }
  if(e.key==='Backspace'){e.preventDefault();backspace();return;}
  if(e.key==='Tab'&&tgt===ghost){e.preventDefault();tab();return;}
  if(e.key==='Unidentified'||e.isComposing)return;
  if(e.key.length===1){
    if(onButton&&e.key===' ')return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if(e.key===' ')space();else typeChar(e.key);
  }
});
ghost.addEventListener('input',()=>{
  const v=ghost.value;ghost.value='';
  for(const ch of v){if(ch==='\n')carriageReturn();else if(ch===' ')space();else typeChar(ch);}
});
function focusGhost(){try{ghost.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(e){ghost.focus();}}
sheet.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{audio();if(e.pointerType!=='mouse')focusGhost();});
$('btnKeyboard').addEventListener('click',()=>{audio();focusGhost();toast('Your phone keyboard now types onto the sheet');});
bodyS.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
  const g=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.key');if(!g)return;
  e.preventDefault();onScreenKey(g.getAttribute('data-k'));
});
const lever=$('lever');
lever.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();carriageReturn();});
lever.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();carriageReturn();}});

/* ---------- controls ---------- */
const btnSound=$('btnSound');
btnSound.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  muted=!muted;audio();if(master)master.gain.value=muted?0:0.6;
  btnSound.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!muted));btnSound.setAttribute('aria-label',muted?'Sound muted — press to unmute':'Sound on — press to mute');
  $('sndLbl').textContent=muted?'Muted':'Sound';$('sndWaves').style.opacity=muted?0.15:1;
  try{localStorage.setItem('marlowe44.muted',muted?'1':'0');}catch(e){}
});
$('btnNew').addEventListener('click',newSheet);
// a mouse click should not leave focus on a control, or the next space bar would press it again
document.querySelectorAll('.controls button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.detail>0)b.blur();}));
let busy=false;
function newSheet(){
  if(busy)return;busy=true;audio();snd.paper();
  sheet.classList.add('pulling');rear.classList.add('pulling');
  setTimeout(()=>{
    sheetNo++;strokes=[];col=0;line=0;bellRung=false;
    redrawAll();
    sheet.classList.remove('pulling');rear.classList.remove('pulling');
    update(true);
    sheet.classList.add('feeding');rear.classList.add('feeding');
    setTimeout(()=>{sheet.classList.remove('feeding');rear.classList.remove('feeding');busy=false;},720);
    persist();toast('Sheet No. '+sheetNo+' is in the machine');
  },reduced?60:760);
}
$('btnSave').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  try{
    paperC.toBlob(b=>{
      if(!b){toast('This browser would not hand over the page');return;}
      const u=makeObj.call(URL,b),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download='marlowe44-letter-sheet'+sheetNo+'.png';
      document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>dropObj.call(URL,u),5000);
      toast('Letter saved as a PNG — <b>ready to post</b>');
    },'image/png');
  }catch(e){toast('This browser would not hand over the page');}
});
const makeObj=URL.createObjectURL,dropObj=URL.revokeObjectURL;

/* ---------- status, toast, persistence ---------- */
let toastT=0;
function toast(html){const t=$('toast');t.innerHTML=html;t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastT);toastT=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2600);}
function announce(s){$('announce').textContent=s;}
function status(){
  $('stSheet').textContent='No. '+sheetNo;
  $('stLine').textContent=Math.min(line+1,LINES)+' / '+LINES;
  $('stCol').textContent=Math.min(col+1,COLS+1)+' / '+COLS;
  $('stCount').textContent=strokes.length;
}
let saveT=0;
function persist(){clearTimeout(saveT);saveT=setTimeout(()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify({strokes:strokes.slice(-3000),col,line,red,sheetNo,bellRung}));}catch(e){}},350);}
function restore(){
  try{
    const raw=localStorage.getItem(STORE);if(!raw)return false;
    const d=JSON.parse(raw);if(!d||!Array.isArray(d.strokes))return false;
    strokes=d.strokes.filter(s=>Array.isArray(s)&&s.length===5);col=d.col|0;line=d.line|0;red=!!d.red;sheetNo=d.sheetNo|0||1;bellRung=!!d.bellRung;
    return true;
  }catch(e){return false;}
}
function openingLines(){
  // the machine arrives with the date and a salutation already on the sheet
  const now=new Date();
  const months=['January','February','March','April','May','June','July','August','September','October','November','December'];
  const date=now.getDate()+' '+months[now.getMonth()]+' '+now.getFullYear();
  let seed=424242;
  const put=(txt,c0,l)=>{for(let i=0;i<txt.length;i++){if(txt[i]!==' ')strokes.push([txt[i],c0+i,l,0,seed+=7919]);}};
  put(date,COLS-date.length,0);
  put('Dear friend,',0,2);
  put('Forgive the smudges; the Marlowe and I are',5,4);
  put('still getting acquainted.',0,5);
  col=26;line=5;
}

/* ---------- boot ---------- */

try{muted=localStorage.getItem('marlowe44.muted')==='1';}catch(e){}
if(muted){btnSound.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('sndLbl').textContent='Muted';$('sndWaves').style.opacity=0.15;btnSound.setAttribute('aria-label','Sound muted — press to unmute');}
if(!restore())openingLines();
$('inkBlack').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!red));$('inkRed').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(red));
$('vibrator').setAttribute('transform',red?'translate(0 -3.8)':'translate(0 0)');
redrawAll();
layout();
startLoop();

})();

================================================================
