## 029 Carriage Return — 1:1 Yantra Port of 029-typewriter-letters.html (Marlowe 44)
##
## Architecture:
##   - Full-viewport interactive Skia `Draw` canvas (`1280x816`) inside a `stack`
##     overlay container, paired with native Yantra header controls and shipping
##     tag nodes for keyboard/focus/test-harness compatibility.
##   - Two persistent raster layers (`deskLayer` for the walnut desk, lamplight,
##     bloom, and dried coffee ring; `paperLayer` at 2x resolution for the cotton
##     bond sheet, watermark, and accumulated ink-stamped characters).
##   - Exact cubic-bezier spring/overshoot solver for carriage escapement snap,
##     carriage return glide, paper line feed, lever pull, and sheet swap.

import yantra
import std/[math, strutils]

# ───────────────────────────────────────────── 1. Constants & Palette
const
  # Paper geometry in paper units (600 x 776, rasterised at 2x into paperLayer)
  PW    = 600.0'f32
  PH    = 776.0'f32
  ML    = 60.0'f32
  TM    = 76.0'f32
  CW    = 10.0'f32
  LH    = 26.0'f32
  COLS  = 48
  LINES = 26
  FS    = 16.6'f32

  # Viewport geometry (desktop reference frame)
  SW    = 1280.0'f32
  SH    = 816.0'f32
  FW    = 1280.0'f32
  VH    = 816.0'f32

  # Typography metric: SkParagraph places `y` at top of line box; baseline is +1.0833*S
  BaselineK = 1.0833'f32
  DegToRad  = 0.0174532925'f32
  RadToDeg  = 57.2957795'f32

  # Source palette (:root in 029-typewriter-letters.html) — all with 0xFF alpha
  cEnamel     = 0xFF9FC9B6'u32
  cEnamelDeep = 0xFF6F9D88'u32
  cCream      = 0xFFF3EAD3'u32
  cPaper      = 0xFFFAF6EC'u32
  cInk        = 0xFF231F1C'u32
  cRed        = 0xFFB3261E'u32
  cChrome     = 0xFFD7DCDF'u32
  cWoodDark   = 0xFF2B170B'u32
  cLamp       = 0xFFFFD79A'u32
  cBody       = 0xFF3A2212'u32
  cCardTop    = 0xFFFDF9EF'u32
  cCardBot    = 0xFFF2EAD6'u32
  cCardInk    = 0xFF2C2622'u32
  cRule       = 0xFF5A82BE'u32
  cTagHole    = 0xFF3A2212'u32

  # SVG path definitions from the HTML source
  P_SIDE       = "M900 16 L936 30 L992 292 Q994 306 978 312 L964 314 Z"
  P_SHELL      = "M96 16 Q102 0 132 0 H868 Q898 0 904 16 L968 296 Q974 330 938 332 H62 Q26 330 32 296 Z"
  P_DECK       = "M104 12 Q108 4 132 4 H868 Q892 4 896 12 L906 70 Q700 88 500 88 Q300 88 94 70 Z"
  P_BASKET     = "M330 4 Q330 74 500 76 Q670 74 670 4 Z"
  P_WELL       = "M86 128 Q88 120 104 120 H896 Q912 120 914 128 L944 300 Q946 314 930 314 H70 Q54 314 56 300 Z"
  P_PLATEN     = "M150 34 Q500 14 850 34 L850 44 L150 44Z"
  P_GLOSS      = "M40 300 Q30 250 70 140"
  P_BAIL_L     = "M116 63.2 Q104 63.2 100 74"
  P_BAIL_R     = "M884 63.2 Q896 63.2 900 74"
  P_TYPEBARS_L = "M500 70L352 8M500 70L364 8M500 70L377 8M500 70L390 7M500 70L403 7M500 70L416 6M500 70L429 6M500 70L442 6M500 70L455 5M500 70L468 5M500 70L481 5M500 70L494 5"
  P_TYPEBARS_R = "M500 70L648 8M500 70L636 8M500 70L623 8M500 70L610 7M500 70L597 7M500 70L584 6M500 70L571 6M500 70L558 6M500 70L545 5M500 70L532 5M500 70L519 5M500 70L506 5"
  LEVER_ARM    = "M84 42 C70 34 44 20 20 8"
  LEVER_PULL_MS = 500.0'f32

# ───────────────────────────────────── 2. Deterministic PRNG & Colour Math
type Rng = object
  a: uint32

proc rngInit(seed: int32): Rng =
  Rng(a: uint32(seed) and 0x7FFFFFFF'u32)

proc nextF(r: var Rng): float32 =
  r.a = r.a + 0x6D2B79F5'u32
  var t = r.a
  t = (t xor (t shr 15)) * (1'u32 or t)
  t = t + ((t xor (t shr 7)) * (61'u32 or t)) xor t
  result = (t xor (t shr 14)).float32 / 4294967296.0'f32

proc rnd32(x: float32): int32 =
  ## Safe integer rounding without std/math.round (which recurses in wasm mini-libc).
  int32(if x < 0.0'f32: x - 0.5'f32 else: x + 0.5'f32)

proc clampF(x, lo, hi: float32): float32 =
  if x < lo: lo elif x > hi: hi else: x

proc rgba(r, g, b: int32; a: float32): uint32 =
  let av = clamp(rnd32(a * 255.0'f32), 0'i32, 255'i32)
  let rv = clamp(r, 0'i32, 255'i32)
  let gv = clamp(g, 0'i32, 255'i32)
  let bv = clamp(b, 0'i32, 255'i32)
  (uint32(av) shl 24) or (uint32(rv) shl 16) or (uint32(gv) shl 8) or uint32(bv)

proc alphaOf(c: uint32): float32 =
  float32((c shr 24) and 0xFF'u32) / 255.0'f32

proc rgbOf(c: uint32): array[3, int32] =
  [int32((c shr 16) and 0xFF'u32), int32((c shr 8) and 0xFF'u32), int32(c and 0xFF'u32)]

proc withA(c: uint32; a: float32): uint32 =
  (c and 0x00FFFFFF'u32) or (uint32(clamp(rnd32(a * 255.0'f32), 0'i32, 255'i32)) shl 24)

proc scaleA(c: uint32; k: float32): uint32 =
  withA(c, clampF(alphaOf(c) * k, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))

proc lerpC(a, b: uint32; t: float32): uint32 =
  let u = clampF(t, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  let ca = rgbOf(a)
  let cb = rgbOf(b)
  let aa = alphaOf(a)
  let ab = alphaOf(b)
  rgba(ca[0] + rnd32(float32(cb[0] - ca[0]) * u),
       ca[1] + rnd32(float32(cb[1] - ca[1]) * u),
       ca[2] + rnd32(float32(cb[2] - ca[2]) * u),
       aa + (ab - aa) * u)

# ──────────────────────────── 3. Typography & Vector Gradient Primitives
type GStop = tuple[o: float32, c: uint32]

const
  chromeStops: array[5, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, 0xFFFBFCFC'u32),
    (0.35'f32, 0xFFB3BABE'u32),
    (0.55'f32, 0xFFEEF1F2'u32),
    (0.80'f32, 0xFF7E878C'u32),
    (1.00'f32, 0xFFC9CED1'u32)
  ]
  chromeHStops: array[5, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, 0xFF7E878C'u32),
    (0.30'f32, 0xFFF6F8F8'u32),
    (0.55'f32, 0xFFAAB2B6'u32),
    (0.80'f32, 0xFFE9ECEE'u32),
    (1.00'f32, 0xFF6F777C'u32)
  ]
  railStops: array[4, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, 0xFFF3F5F6'u32),
    (0.45'f32, 0xFF9AA2A7'u32),
    (0.60'f32, 0xFFE1E5E7'u32),
    (1.00'f32, 0xFF6C747A'u32)
  ]
  rimStops: array[4, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, 0xFFFFFFFF'u32),
    (0.40'f32, 0xFFA7AFB4'u32),
    (0.62'f32, 0xFFE8EBED'u32),
    (1.00'f32, 0xFF5F676C'u32)
  ]
  rearPaperStops: array[3, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, 0xFFEFE8D6'u32),
    (0.60'f32, 0xFFE7DFCB'u32),
    (1.00'f32, 0xFFCFC6B0'u32)
  ]

proc gradRamp(stops: openArray[GStop]; t: float32): uint32 =
  if t <= stops[0].o: return stops[0].c
  let n = stops.len
  if t >= stops[n - 1].o: return stops[n - 1].c
  for i in 0 ..< n - 1:
    if t <= stops[i + 1].o:
      let span = stops[i + 1].o - stops[i].o
      let f = if span <= 0.0'f32: 0.0'f32 else: (t - stops[i].o) / span
      return lerpC(stops[i].c, stops[i + 1].c, f)
  stops[n - 1].c

proc utf8CodePoints(s: string): int =
  ## Pure UTF-8 code-point count without importing std/unicode.
  for ch in s:
    if (ord(ch) and 0xC0) != 0x80:
      inc result

proc tFace*(d: Draw; x, y, size: float32; col: uint32; family, s: string;
           weight = 400): Draw =
  ## Host-compat shim: shipped native host renders NOTHING for the N verb,
  ## so family text falls back to the default-face T verb (identical origin).
  d.text(x, y, size, col, s, weight)

proc measureAdv(s: string; size: float32; family = "Courier New"; weight = 400): float32 =
  ## No `textWidth`: shipped host lacks `y_text_measure` (aborts WASM at launch).
  ## Deterministic 0.6001em Courier New monospace advance.
  if s.len == 0: return 0.0'f32
  float32(utf8CodePoints(s)) * size * 0.6001'f32

proc textBaseline(d: Draw; x, baseY, size: float32; argb: uint32;
                  family, s: string; weight = 400): Draw =
  ## Draws text with `baseY` placed on the alphabetic baseline.
  d.tFace(x, baseY - BaselineK * size, size, argb, family, s, weight)

proc textCentered(d: Draw; centerX, baseY, size: float32; argb: uint32;
                  family, s: string; weight = 400): Draw =
  ## Equivalent to SVG `text-anchor="middle"` + `textBaseline="alphabetic"`.
  let w = measureAdv(s, size, family, weight)
  d.tFace(centerX - w * 0.5'f32, baseY - BaselineK * size, size, argb, family, s, weight)

proc ellipsePath(x, y, rx, ry: float32): string =
  "M" & $(x - rx) & " " & $y &
  "a" & $rx & " " & $ry & " 0 1 0 " & $(rx * 2.0'f32) & " 0" &
  "a" & $rx & " " & $ry & " 0 1 0 " & $(-rx * 2.0'f32) & " 0Z"

proc gradRectV(d: var Draw; x, y, w, h, radius: float32; stops: openArray[GStop];
               opacity = 1.0'f32; n = 24) =
  for k in 0 ..< n:
    let c = gradRamp(stops, (float32(k) + 0.5'f32) / float32(n))
    d = d.save()
    d = d.clip(x - 0.75'f32, y + h * float32(k) / float32(n) - 0.75'f32,
               w + 1.5'f32, h / float32(n) + 1.5'f32)
    d = d.rect(x, y, w, h, withA(c, opacity), radius)
    d = d.restore()

proc gradRectH(d: var Draw; x, y, w, h, radius: float32; stops: openArray[GStop];
               opacity = 1.0'f32; n = 12) =
  for k in 0 ..< n:
    let c = gradRamp(stops, (float32(k) + 0.5'f32) / float32(n))
    d = d.save()
    d = d.clip(x + w * float32(k) / float32(n) - 0.75'f32, y - 0.75'f32,
               w / float32(n) + 1.5'f32, h + 1.5'f32)
    d = d.rect(x, y, w, h, withA(c, opacity), radius)
    d = d.restore()

proc gradShapeV(d: var Draw; path: string; x, y, sc: float32;
                gy0, gy1, dx0, dx1: float32; stops: openArray[GStop];
                opacity = 1.0'f32; n = 64) =
  for k in 0 ..< n:
    let u0 = gy0 + (gy1 - gy0) * float32(k) / float32(n)
    let u1 = gy0 + (gy1 - gy0) * float32(k + 1) / float32(n)
    let c = gradRamp(stops, (float32(k) + 0.5'f32) / float32(n))
    d = d.save()
    d = d.clip(dx0, y + sc * u0 - 0.75'f32, dx1 - dx0, sc * (u1 - u0) + 1.5'f32)
    d = d.svgPath(path, x, y, sc, withA(c, opacity))
    d = d.restore()

proc gradShapeH(d: var Draw; path: string; x, y, sc: float32;
                gx0, gx1, dy0, dy1: float32; stops: openArray[GStop];
                opacity = 1.0'f32; n = 48) =
  for k in 0 ..< n:
    let u0 = gx0 + (gx1 - gx0) * float32(k) / float32(n)
    let u1 = gx0 + (gx1 - gx0) * float32(k + 1) / float32(n)
    let c = gradRamp(stops, (float32(k) + 0.5'f32) / float32(n))
    d = d.save()
    d = d.clip(x + sc * u0 - 0.75'f32, dy0, sc * (u1 - u0) + 1.5'f32, dy1 - dy0)
    d = d.svgPath(path, x, y, sc, withA(c, opacity))
    d = d.restore()

proc radialShape(d: var Draw; path: string; x, y, sc: float32;
                 x0, y0, x1, y1, gx, gy, grx, gry: float32;
                 stops: openArray[GStop]; n = 12; opacity = 1.0'f32) =
  let fn = float32(n)
  for ri in 0 ..< n:
    let cyp = y0 + (y1 - y0) * (float32(ri) + 0.5'f32) / fn
    let v = (cyp - gy) / gry
    for ci in 0 ..< n:
      let cxp = x0 + (x1 - x0) * (float32(ci) + 0.5'f32) / fn
      let u = (cxp - gx) / grx
      d = d.save()
      d = d.clip(x0 + (x1 - x0) * float32(ci) / fn - 0.75'f32,
                 y0 + (y1 - y0) * float32(ri) / fn - 0.75'f32,
                 (x1 - x0) / fn + 1.5'f32, (y1 - y0) / fn + 1.5'f32)
      d = d.svgPath(path, x, y, sc, withA(gradRamp(stops, sqrt(u * u + v * v)), opacity))
      d = d.restore()

# ─────────────────────────────────────────── 4. Application State & Globals
type Stroke = object
  ch: char
  col, line: int32
  red: uint8
  seed: int32

type Mote = object
  x, y, z, s, p: float32

var
  col = 0
  line = 0
  sheetNo = 1
  red = false
  bellRung = false
  shiftOn = false
  lockOn = false
  muted = false
  strokes: seq[Stroke] = @[]
  motes: seq[Mote] = @[]
  seedCounter = 1

  # Machine layout metrics
  MW = 0.0'f32
  U = 1.0'f32
  MX = 0.0'f32
  MY = 0.0'f32
  S = 1.0'f32
  X0 = 0.0'f32
  PRINTY = 0.0'f32

  # Animation clocks & states
  nowMs = 0.0'f32
  frameSec = 0.0'f32
  carX = 0.0'f32
  paperY = 0.0'f32
  bellFlash = 0.0'f32
  strikeFlash = 0.0'f32
  dingT = -1.0'f32
  leverT = -1.0'f32
  toastMsg = ""
  toastT = 0.0'f32
  litKey = ""
  litKeyTimer = 0.0'f32
  hoverKey = ""
  lastCursor = 0'i32

  # Draggable desk props (0 at rest for pixel-exact initial frame)
  pencilDX = 0.0'f32
  pencilDY = 0.0'f32
  cardDX = 0.0'f32
  cardDY = 0.0'f32
  dragProp = ""
  dragOX = 0.0'f32
  dragOY = 0.0'f32
  downPX = 0.0'f32
  downPY = 0.0'f32
  propMoved = false

  # Status strings & UI handles
  statusSheet = ""
  statusLine = ""
  statusCol = ""
  statusCount = ""
  deskLayer: Layer
  paperLayer: Layer
  scene: Node
  sceneReady = false
  tagSheet, tagLine, tagColN, tagCount: Node
  tagReady = false
  bBlack, bRed: Node
  ribbonBtnsReady = false
  sndBell, sndKey, sndPaper, sndRet, sndSoft, sndSpace: Sound

proc cx(x: float32): float32 = MX + x * U
proc cy(y: float32): float32 = MY + y * U
proc by(y: float32): float32 = MY + 100.0'f32 * U + y * U

# ─────────────────────── 5. Procedural Walnut Desk & Lamplight Engine
const
  plankTones: array[4, array[3, int32]] = [
    [92'i32, 52'i32, 26'i32],
    [104'i32, 60'i32, 31'i32],
    [86'i32, 48'i32, 24'i32],
    [98'i32, 56'i32, 28'i32]
  ]
  lampStops: array[4, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, rgba(255, 214, 150, 0.50'f32)),
    (0.35'f32, rgba(255, 190, 120, 0.20'f32)),
    (0.75'f32, rgba(40,  18,  6,   0.15'f32)),
    (1.00'f32, rgba(10,  4,   1,   0.62'f32))
  ]

proc drawKnotRing(d: Draw; kx, ky: float32; q: int32): Draw =
  let rad = q.float32 * 7.0'f32
  let ry = q.float32 * 2.2'f32
  let wob = 1.0'f32 + sin(q.float32 * 1.7'f32) * 0.06'f32
  result = d.save()
  var deg = 0.0'f32
  while deg <= 360.0'f32:
    let modW = 1.0'f32 + sin(deg * 0.0596'f32 + q.float32) * 0.06'f32
    let px = kx + cos(deg * DegToRad) * rad * modW * wob
    let py = ky + sin(deg * DegToRad) * ry * modW
    result = if deg == 0.0'f32: result.moveTo(px, py) else: result.lineTo(px, py)
    deg += 9.0'f32
  result = result.close().stroke(rgba(40, 18, 6, 0.05'f32 + q.float32 * 0.012'f32), 1.0'f32).restore()

proc drawCoffeeRing(d: Draw; cxp, cyp: float32): Draw =
  result = d
  for i in 0 ..< 3:
    let k = i.float32
    result = result.save()
    var deg = 0.0'f32
    while deg <= 372.0'f32:
      let wob = sin(deg * 0.0122'f32 + k * 2.1'f32) * 0.028'f32 + sin(deg * 0.0291'f32 + k) * 0.014'f32
      let rr = 44.0'f32 * (1.0'f32 + wob)
      let px = cxp + cos(deg * DegToRad) * rr
      let py = cyp + sin(deg * DegToRad) * rr * 0.94'f32
      result = if deg == 0.0'f32: result.moveTo(px, py) else: result.lineTo(px, py)
      deg += 6.0'f32
    let ringCol = if i == 0: rgba(60, 28, 8, 0.22'f32)
                  elif i == 1: rgba(60, 28, 8, 0.14'f32)
                  else: rgba(74, 38, 12, 0.07'f32)
    result = result.close().stroke(ringCol, if i == 0: 3.0'f32 else: 1.0'f32).restore()
  result = result.save().circle(cxp + 2.0'f32, cyp + 1.0'f32, 39.0'f32, rgba(72, 36, 10, 0.045'f32)).restore()

proc drawPlankBody(d: var Draw; r: var Rng; w, y, ph: float32; idx: int) =
  let t = plankTones[idx mod 4]
  let hi = rgba(t[0] + 6, t[1] + 4, t[2] + 2, 1.0'f32)
  let lo = rgba(t[0] - 8, t[1] - 6, t[2] - 4, 1.0'f32)
  for band in 0 ..< 24:
    let f0 = band.float32 / 24.0'f32
    let f1 = (band + 1).float32 / 24.0'f32
    d = d.rect(0.0'f32, y + ph * f0, w, ph * (f1 - f0) + 1.0'f32, lerpC(hi, lo, (f0 + f1) * 0.5'f32))

  let n = rnd32(ph / 2.4'f32)
  for _ in 0 ..< n:
    let gy = y + r.nextF() * ph
    let amp = 1.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 5.0'f32
    let fq = 0.0016'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.005'f32
    let p0 = r.nextF() * 6.28318'f32
    let dark = r.nextF() < 0.62'f32
    let a = (if dark: 0.08'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.20'f32 else: 0.05'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.12'f32) * 0.75'f32
    let grainCol = if dark: rgba(38, 18, 6, a) else: rgba(176, 112, 60, a)
    let lw = 0.5'f32 + r.nextF() * 1.7'f32
    d = d.save()
    var x = -12.0'f32
    var started = false
    while x <= w + 12.0'f32:
      let yy = gy + sin(x * fq + p0) * amp + sin(x * fq * 3.3'f32 + p0 * 2.0'f32) * amp * 0.35'f32
      d = if started: d.lineTo(x, yy) else: d.moveTo(x, yy)
      started = true
      x += 12.0'f32
    d = d.stroke(grainCol, lw).restore()

  if r.nextF() < 0.55'f32:
    let kx = r.nextF() * w
    let ky = y + ph * (0.30'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.40'f32)
    for q in countdown(10, 1):
      d = drawKnotRing(d, kx, ky, q.int32)
    d = d.save().circle(kx, ky, 5.0'f32, rgba(26, 11, 4, 0.34'f32)).restore()

  d = d.rect(0.0'f32, y + ph - 2.0'f32, w, 2.0'f32, rgba(18, 8, 2, 0.60'f32))
  d = d.rect(0.0'f32, y + ph, w, 1.0'f32, rgba(255, 196, 130, 0.08'f32))

proc paintDesk() =
  var r = rngInit(897)
  var d = Draw().rect(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, FW, VH, cWoodDark)
  var y = -20.0'f32
  var idx = 0
  while y < VH:
    let ph = 190.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 80.0'f32
    drawPlankBody(d, r, FW, y, ph, idx)
    y += ph
    inc idx

  # Upper-left lamplight radial (96 concentric stroked rings)
  let lx = FW * 0.18'f32
  let ly = -VH * 0.12'f32
  let lr = sqrt(FW * FW + VH * VH) * 0.95'f32
  d = d.save().blend(bSrcOver)
  for i in 0 ..< 96:
    let t0 = i.float32 / 96.0'f32
    let t1 = (i + 1).float32 / 96.0'f32
    let mid = (t0 + t1) * 0.5'f32
    let c = gradRamp(lampStops, mid)
    if alphaOf(c) > 0.0008'f32:
      d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(lx, ly, lr * mid, lr * mid), 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, c, lr * (t1 - t0))
  d = d.restore()

  # Warm bloom behind the machine (48 concentric stroked rings)
  let bx = FW * 0.50'f32
  let byc = VH * 0.62'f32
  let br = max(FW, VH) * 0.50'f32
  d = d.save().blend(bSrcOver)
  for i in 0 ..< 48:
    let t0 = i.float32 / 48.0'f32
    let t1 = (i + 1).float32 / 48.0'f32
    let mid = (t0 + t1) * 0.5'f32
    d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(bx, byc, br * mid, br * mid), 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32,
                  rgba(255, 200, 130, 0.14'f32 * (1.0'f32 - mid)), br * (t1 - t0))
  d = d.restore()

  d = drawCoffeeRing(d, FW * 0.90'f32, VH * 0.30'f32)
  deskLayer.clear()
  deskLayer.draw(d)

# ───────────────── 6. Procedural Cotton Paper & Character Stamping Engine
const
  inkBlackRgb = [35'i32, 31'i32, 28'i32]
  inkRedRgb   = [176'i32, 36'i32, 28'i32]

proc buildPaper(sheet: int32): Draw =
  var r = rngInit(sheet * 7919 + 13)
  var d = Draw().scale(2.0'f32, 2.0'f32).rect(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, PW, PH, cPaper)

  # 60 cloudy formation patches
  const cloudDensity = 0.14'f32
  for _ in 0 ..< 60:
    let x = r.nextF() * PW
    let y = r.nextF() * PH
    let rad = 30.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 90.0'f32
    let dark = r.nextF() < 0.5'f32
    let peak = if dark: 0.07'f32 else: 0.10'f32
    let base = if dark: rgba(214, 200, 170, peak) else: rgba(255, 255, 250, peak)
    const STEPS = 6
    for s in countdown(STEPS - 1, 0):
      let k = float32(STEPS - 1 - s)
      let rr = rad * (1.0'f32 - (k / float32(STEPS - 1)) * 0.95'f32)
      let a = (1.0'f32 - pow(1.0'f32 - peak, 1.0'f32 / float32(STEPS))) * cloudDensity
      d = d.circle(x, y, rr, scaleA(base, a / peak))

  # 900 curved cotton fibres
  for _ in 0 ..< 900:
    let x = r.nextF() * PW
    let y = r.nextF() * PH
    let a = r.nextF() * 3.14159265'f32
    let l = 2.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 9.0'f32
    let warm = r.nextF() < 0.7'f32
    let c = if warm: rgba(160, 140, 105, 0.05'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.08'f32)
            else: rgba(120, 135, 160, 0.04'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.05'f32)
    let lw = 0.3'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.4'f32
    d = d.save().moveTo(x, y)
         .quadTo(x + cos(a + 0.6'f32) * l * 0.5'f32,
                 y + sin(a + 0.6'f32) * l * 0.5'f32,
                 x + cos(a) * l, y + sin(a) * l)
         .stroke(c, lw).restore()

  # 5200 speckle grain dots
  for _ in 0 ..< 5200:
    let c = if r.nextF() < 0.5'f32: rgba(120, 100, 70, 0.06'f32)
            else: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18'f32)
    let sz = 0.5'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.6'f32
    d = d.rect(r.nextF() * PW, r.nextF() * PH, sz, sz, c)

  # "Marlowe Bond" watermark centered inside its 122x40 ellipse ring
  let wmCol = rgba(122, 106, 74, 0.045'f32)
  d = d.save().translate(PW * 0.5'f32, PH * 0.62'f32)
  d = d.textCentered(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 30.0'f32, wmCol, "Georgia", "Marlowe Bond", 400)
  d = d.textCentered(0.0'f32, 22.0'f32, 11.0'f32, wmCol, "Courier New", "25% COTTON FIBRE \u00B7 No. 44", 400)
  var deg = 0.0'f32
  while deg <= 360.0'f32:
    let px = cos(deg * DegToRad) * 122.0'f32
    let py = sin(deg * DegToRad) * 40.0'f32 - 8.0'f32
    d = if deg == 0.0'f32: d.moveTo(px, py) else: d.lineTo(px, py)
    deg += 4.0'f32
  d = d.close().stroke(wmCol, 1.0'f32).restore()

  # Edge darkening + deckle shading
  const STEPS = 10
  for e in 0 ..< STEPS:
    let bandX = PW * 0.04'f32 / STEPS.float32
    let bandY = PH * 0.03'f32 / STEPS.float32
    let mid = (e.float32 + 0.5'f32) / STEPS.float32
    let aX   = 0.10'f32 * (1.0'f32 - mid)
    let aTop = 0.10'f32 * (1.0'f32 - mid)
    let aBot = 0.12'f32 * (1.0'f32 - mid)
    let ox = e.float32 * bandX
    let oy = e.float32 * bandY
    d = d.rect(ox, 0.0'f32, bandX, PH, rgba(150, 125, 85, aX))
    d = d.rect(PW - ox - bandX, 0.0'f32, bandX, PH, rgba(150, 125, 85, aX))
    d = d.rect(0.0'f32, oy, PW, bandY, rgba(150, 125, 85, aTop))
    d = d.rect(0.0'f32, PH - oy - bandY, PW, bandY, rgba(150, 125, 85, aBot))
  result = d

proc isHeavyGlyph(ch: char): bool =
  (ch >= 'A' and ch <= 'Z') or (ch >= '0' and ch <= '9') or ch in {'#', '%', '&', '@', '$'}

proc strokeDensity(ch: char; c, l, seed: int32; r: var Rng): float32 =
  var dens = 0.80'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.20'f32
  if r.nextF() < 0.07'f32:
    dens *= 0.58'f32
  let wear = 0.90'f32 + 0.10'f32 * sin(c.float32 * 0.31'f32 + l.float32 * 1.7'f32 + float32(seed mod 7))
  dens *= wear
  if isHeavyGlyph(ch):
    dens = min(1.0'f32, dens * 1.06'f32)
  clampF(dens, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)

proc appendGlyphStamp(d: var Draw; s: Stroke) =
  if s.ch == ' ': return
  var r = rngInit(s.seed)
  let rgb = if s.red == 1'u8: inkRedRgb else: inkBlackRgb
  let dens = strokeDensity(s.ch, s.col, s.line, s.seed, r)
  let x = ML + s.col.float32 * CW + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 0.9'f32
  let y = TM + s.line.float32 * LH + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 1.7'f32
  let rotDeg = (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 0.045'f32 * RadToDeg
  let dblRoll = r.nextF()
  let weaveOff = r.nextF()
  let platenRoll = r.nextF()
  let inkC = rgba(rgb[0], rgb[1], rgb[2], min(1.0'f32, dens))
  let chStr = $s.ch
  let halfW = measureAdv(chStr, FS, "Courier New", 700) * 0.5'f32
  let baseOffY = -BaselineK * FS

  proc stampPlate(dd: Draw; px, py, alpha: float32): Draw =
    ## dd already carries the 2x paper-unit scale from stampOne: translate +
    ## rotate only (a second .scale here would 4x the coords and throw every
    ## glyph off the 1200x1552 layer = invisible ink).
    var pl = dd.translate(px, py).rotate(rotDeg)
    # 1. Ink bleed halo (matches HTML shadowBlur = 1.6)
    for k in 0 ..< 4:
      let sc = 1.0'f32 + k.float32 * 0.055'f32
      let a = alpha * (0.16'f32 - k.float32 * 0.035'f32)
      pl = pl.save().scale(sc)
      pl = pl.tFace(-halfW, baseOffY, FS, rgba(rgb[0], rgb[1], rgb[2], a), "Courier New", chStr, 700)
      pl = pl.restore()
    # 2. Core character strike centered horizontally and seated on baseline
    pl = pl.save()
    pl = pl.tFace(-halfW, baseOffY, FS, scaleA(inkC, alpha), "Courier New", chStr, 700)
    pl = pl.restore()
    # 3. Fabric ribbon weave knockout
    let wipe = 0.30'f32 + (1.0'f32 - dens) * 0.90'f32
    var wx = -weaveOff * FS * 1.2'f32
    var wy = -weaveOff * FS * 0.8'f32 - FS * 0.5'f32
    while wx < FS * 1.1'f32:
      pl = pl.blend(eraser)
             .line(wx, wy, wx + FS * 0.9'f32, wy + FS * 0.5'f32,
                   rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16'f32 * wipe), 0.7'f32)
             .blend(bSrcOver)
      wx += FS * 0.42'f32
      wy += FS * 0.30'f32
    # 4. Uneven platen contact (40% of strikes print lighter on top or bottom half)
    if platenRoll < 0.40'f32:
      let topHalf = platenRoll < 0.20'f32
      pl = pl.blend(eraser)
      for b in 0 ..< 5:
        let f = b.float32 / 5.0'f32
        let yTop = if topHalf: -FS + FS * 0.5'f32 * f else: -FS * 0.2'f32 - FS * 0.5'f32 * f
        let a = (0.25'f32 + platenRoll * 0.70'f32) * (1.0'f32 - f) * 0.5'f32
        pl = pl.rect(-FS * 1.2'f32, yTop, FS * 2.4'f32, FS * 0.5'f32, rgba(0, 0, 0, a))
      pl = pl.blend(bSrcOver)
    result = pl

  d = d.save().blend(bMultiply)
  d = stampPlate(d, x + CW * 0.5'f32, y, min(1.0'f32, dens))
  if dblRoll < 0.06'f32:
    d = stampPlate(d, x + CW * 0.5'f32 + 0.9'f32, y + 0.35'f32, dens * 0.30'f32)
  d = d.restore()

proc paintPaper(sheet: int32) =
  paperLayer.clear()
  paperLayer.draw(buildPaper(sheet))

proc stampOne(s: Stroke) =
  if s.ch == ' ': return
  var d = Draw().scale(2.0'f32, 2.0'f32)
  appendGlyphStamp(d, s)
  paperLayer.draw(d)

proc redrawAll() =
  paintPaper(sheetNo.int32)
  for st in strokes:
    stampOne(st)

# ────────────────────────────── 7. Cubic-Bezier Physics & Layout
type Ease = object
  p: array[4, float32]

type Anim = object
  fromPos, toPos, elapsed, dur: float32
  ease: Ease
  done: bool

const
  easeSnap    = Ease(p: [0.30'f32, 1.50'f32, 0.55'f32, 1.00'f32])
  easeGlide   = Ease(p: [0.22'f32, 0.90'f32, 0.30'f32, 1.06'f32])
  easeFeed    = Ease(p: [0.30'f32, 1.30'f32, 0.50'f32, 1.00'f32])
  easePull    = Ease(p: [0.30'f32, 0.90'f32, 0.40'f32, 1.00'f32])
  easePullOut = Ease(p: [0.55'f32, 0.00'f32, 0.80'f32, 0.40'f32])
  easeFeedIn  = Ease(p: [0.20'f32, 0.80'f32, 0.30'f32, 1.00'f32])

var
  carAnim, paperAnim: Anim
  returning = false
  returnT = 0.0'f32
  sheetT = -1.0'f32
  sheetSwapped = false
  sheetBusy = false

proc bezier(e: Ease; x: float32): float32 =
  let xc = clampF(x, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  var lo = 0.0'f32
  var hi = 1.0'f32
  var t = xc
  for _ in 0 ..< 28:
    let u = 1.0'f32 - t
    let bx = 3.0'f32 * u * u * t * e.p[0] + 3.0'f32 * u * t * t * e.p[2] + t * t * t
    if bx < xc: lo = t else: hi = t
    t = (lo + hi) * 0.5'f32
  let u = 1.0'f32 - t
  3.0'f32 * u * u * t * e.p[1] + 3.0'f32 * u * t * t * e.p[3] + t * t * t

proc start(a: var Anim; fromPos, toPos, dur: float32; ease: Ease) =
  a.fromPos = fromPos
  a.toPos = toPos
  a.dur = dur
  a.ease = ease
  a.elapsed = 0.0'f32
  a.done = abs(toPos - fromPos) < 0.01'f32

proc advance(a: var Anim; dtMs: float32): float32 =
  if not a.done:
    a.elapsed += dtMs
    if a.elapsed >= a.dur:
      a.elapsed = a.dur
      a.done = true
  a.fromPos + (a.toPos - a.fromPos) * bezier(a.ease, a.elapsed / max(a.dur, 1.0'f32))

proc layout() =
  let hFit = (SH * 0.46'f32) / 0.45'f32
  MW = min(920.0'f32, min(SW * 0.64'f32, hFit))
  U = MW / 1000.0'f32
  MX = (SW - MW) * 0.5'f32
  MY = SH - 450.0'f32 * U - 10.0'f32
  S = 0.60'f32 * MW / PW
  X0 = SW * 0.5'f32
  PRINTY = MY + 80.0'f32 * U

proc paperLeftFor(c: float32): float32 =
  X0 - (ML + c * CW) * S

proc updateCarriage(instant: bool) =
  let cc = float32(min(col, COLS))
  let left = paperLeftFor(cc)
  let feed = PRINTY - (TM + line.float32 * LH) * S
  if instant:
    carX = left
    paperY = feed
    carAnim.start(left, left, 1.0'f32, easeSnap)
    paperAnim.start(feed, feed, 1.0'f32, easeSnap)
  elif returning:
    carAnim.start(carX, left, 460.0'f32, easeGlide)
    paperAnim.start(paperY, feed, 200.0'f32, easeFeed)
  else:
    carAnim.start(carX, left, 70.0'f32, easeSnap)
    paperAnim.start(paperY, feed, 200.0'f32, easeFeed)

proc status() =
  statusSheet = "No. " & $sheetNo
  statusLine = $min(line + 1, LINES) & " / " & $LINES
  statusCol = $min(col + 1, COLS + 1) & " / " & $COLS
  statusCount = $strokes.len
  if tagReady:
    tagSheet.setText(statusSheet)
    tagLine.setText(statusLine)
    tagColN.setText(statusCol)
    tagCount.setText(statusCount)

proc sheetFx(): (float32, float32, float32, float32) =
  if sheetT < 0.0'f32: return (0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  if sheetT < 760.0'f32:
    let p = bezier(easePullOut, sheetT / 760.0'f32)
    return (-979.2'f32 * p, -4.0'f32 * p, -979.2'f32 * p, 1.0'f32)
  let q = bezier(easeFeedIn, min((sheetT - 760.0'f32) / 720.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
  (40.0'f32 * (1.0'f32 - q), 0.0'f32, 40.0'f32 * (1.0'f32 - q), q)

# ──────────────────────────── 8. Platen, Knobs & Desk Props Rendering
const
  platenStops: array[6, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, 0xFF1A1716'u32),
    (0.22'f32, 0xFF4A4441'u32),
    (0.34'f32, 0xFF6D6562'u32),
    (0.46'f32, 0xFF2E2A28'u32),
    (0.80'f32, 0xFF141211'u32),
    (1.00'f32, 0xFF0A0908'u32)
  ]
  knobStops: array[4, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, 0xFF3A3533'u32),
    (0.30'f32, 0xFF6B6461'u32),
    (0.50'f32, 0xFF2A2624'u32),
    (1.00'f32, 0xFF0D0C0B'u32)
  ]
  pencilStops: array[3, GStop] = [
    (0.00'f32, 0xFFF0C24A'u32),
    (0.50'f32, 0xFFD9A11F'u32),
    (1.00'f32, 0xFFA8770F'u32)
  ]

proc platenBarrel(f: float32): float32 =
  let sym = (f - 0.5'f32) * 2.0'f32
  let e = if abs(sym) > 0.62'f32: (abs(sym) - 0.62'f32) / 0.38'f32 else: 0.0'f32
  clampF(f * 0.86'f32 + 0.07'f32 - e * 0.10'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)

proc drawPlaten(d: var Draw; x, y, w, h: float32) =
  let radius = h * 0.147'f32
  const BANDS = 40
  for i in 0 ..< BANDS:
    let f0 = i.float32 / BANDS.float32
    let f1 = (i + 1).float32 / BANDS.float32
    let c = gradRamp(platenStops, platenBarrel((f0 + f1) * 0.5'f32))
    let r = if i == 0 or i == BANDS - 1: radius else: 0.0'f32
    d = d.rect(x, y + h * f0, w, h * (f1 - f0) + 0.6'f32, c, r)

  # Circumferential rubber grain
  var s = 0x2545F491'u32
  for _ in 0 ..< 90:
    s = s * 1664525'u32 + 1013904223'u32
    let fy = float32((s shr 16) and 0xFFFF'u32) / 65536.0'f32
    s = s * 1664525'u32 + 1013904223'u32
    let fx = float32((s shr 16) and 0xFFFF'u32) / 65536.0'f32
    s = s * 1664525'u32 + 1013904223'u32
    let fa = float32((s shr 16) and 0xFFFF'u32) / 65536.0'f32
    let yy = y + h * (0.06'f32 + fy * 0.88'f32)
    let xx = x + w * fx
    let len = w * (0.04'f32 + fa * 0.16'f32)
    let c = if ((s shr 4) and 1'u32) == 0'u32: 0x0EFFFFFF'u32 else: 0x0A000000'u32
    d = d.line(xx, yy, min(xx + len, x + w), yy, c, 0.8'f32)

  # Specular line at y = 58 in carriage space (22/68 of roller height)
  let midY = y + h * (22.0'f32 / 68.0'f32)
  d = d.line(x + w * 0.005'f32, midY, x + w * 0.995'f32, midY, 0x1FFFFFFF'u32, 2.0'f32 * U)

proc drawKnob(d: var Draw; x, y, w, h, radius: float32; grooves: int32) =
  gradRectV(d, x, y, w, h, radius, knobStops, 1.0'f32, 24)
  let gTop = y + h * 0.07'f32
  let gBot = y + h * 0.93'f32
  for g in 0 ..< grooves:
    let f = g.float32 / max(1.0'f32, grooves.float32 - 1.0'f32)
    let gx = x + w * (0.12'f32 + f * 0.76'f32)
    d = d.line(gx, gTop, gx, gBot, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.10'f32), 1.5'f32 * U)

proc rotPt(cxp, cyp, deg, lx, ly: float32): (float32, float32) =
  let r = deg * DegToRad
  let cs = cos(r)
  let sn = sin(r)
  let dx = lx - cxp
  let dy = ly - cyp
  (cxp + dx * cs - dy * sn, cyp + dx * sn + dy * cs)

proc quadPath(pts: array[4, (float32, float32)]): string =
  result = "M" & $pts[0][0] & " " & $pts[0][1]
  for i in 1 .. 3:
    result.add "L" & $pts[i][0] & " " & $pts[i][1]
  result.add "Z"

proc rotQuad(cxp, cyp, deg, lx, ly, w, h: float32): string =
  quadPath([
    rotPt(cxp, cyp, deg, lx, ly),
    rotPt(cxp, cyp, deg, lx + w, ly),
    rotPt(cxp, cyp, deg, lx + w, ly + h),
    rotPt(cxp, cyp, deg, lx, ly + h)
  ])

proc drawCard(d: var Draw) =
  ## Operator's index card (html:117-125, 172-180), rotated -4deg around its centre.
  let cw = 262.0'f32
  let ch = 176.0'f32
  let lx = max(24.0'f32, MX * 0.5'f32 - 140.0'f32) + cardDX
  let ly = SH * 0.44'f32 + cardDY
  let cxp = lx + cw * 0.5'f32
  let cyp = ly + ch * 0.5'f32

  # Soft Gaussian drop shadow (box-shadow: 0 18px 30px -12px rgba(0,0,0,.7))
  const shIn = 12.0'f32
  const shDy = 18.0'f32
  const shRings: array[13, (float32, float32)] = [
    (45.0'f32, 0.000945'f32), (36.0'f32, 0.004798'f32), (30.0'f32, 0.010246'f32),
    (25.0'f32, 0.017812'f32), (21.0'f32, 0.023875'f32), (17.0'f32, 0.035450'f32),
    (14.0'f32, 0.035989'f32), (12.0'f32, 0.029149'f32), ( 9.0'f32, 0.051284'f32),
    ( 6.0'f32, 0.060924'f32), ( 3.0'f32, 0.070261'f32), ( 0.0'f32, 0.078644'f32),
    (-6.0'f32, 0.167377'f32)
  ]
  for s in 0 ..< shRings.len:
    let g = shRings[s][0]
    d = d.svgPath(rotQuad(cxp, cyp, -4.0'f32, lx + shIn - g, ly + shIn + shDy - g,
                          cw - 2.0'f32 * shIn + 2.0'f32 * g,
                          ch - 2.0'f32 * shIn + 2.0'f32 * g),
                  0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, withA(0xFF000000'u32, shRings[s][1]))

  # Rotate card body, rules, and typography together in Skia transform space
  d = d.save().translate(cxp, cyp).rotate(-4.0'f32).translate(-cxp, -cyp)
  gradRectV(d, lx, ly, cw, ch, 0.0'f32, [(0.0'f32, cCardTop), (1.0'f32, cCardBot)], 1.0'f32, 24)

  # Blue horizontal ruled lines every 22px starting at y + 33
  var ruleY = ly + 33.0'f32
  while ruleY < ly + ch - 10.0'f32:
    d = d.line(lx + 1.0'f32, ruleY, lx + cw - 1.0'f32, ruleY, withA(cRule, 0.30'f32), 1.0'f32)
    ruleY += 22.0'f32

  # Red header rule (top: 32px) and left margin line (left: 13px)
  d = d.line(lx + 1.0'f32, ly + 32.0'f32, lx + cw - 1.0'f32, ly + 32.0'f32, rgba(200, 60, 60, 0.60'f32), 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(lx + 13.0'f32, ly + 1.0'f32, lx + 13.0'f32, ly + ch - 1.0'f32, rgba(200, 60, 60, 0.35'f32), 1.0'f32)

  # All 7 lines of operator copy with opaque cCardInk (0xFF2C2622)
  const cardLines: array[7, tuple[txt: string, col: uint32, sz: float32, wt: int]] = [
    ("MARLOWE 44 \u00B7 OPERATOR", cRed,     11.5'f32, 700),
    ("Type away, or tap the keys.", cCardInk, 12.0'f32, 400),
    ("Return / lever: new line.",   cCardInk, 12.0'f32, 400),
    ("Backspace steps back.",       cCardInk, 12.0'f32, 400),
    ("(It never erases a thing.)",  cCardInk, 12.0'f32, 400),
    ("Tab jumps five spaces.",      cCardInk, 12.0'f32, 400),
    ("Bell: six from the margin.",  cCardInk, 12.0'f32, 400)
  ]
  for i in 0 ..< cardLines.len:
    let item = cardLines[i]
    let baseY = ly + 12.0'f32 + 22.0'f32 * float32(i) + 16.0'f32
    d = d.textBaseline(lx + 20.0'f32, baseY, item.sz, item.col, "Courier New", item.txt, item.wt)
  d = d.restore()

proc drawPencil(d: var Draw) =
  ## Yellow wooden pencil (html:181-188), 210x14 rotated -24deg as one coherent unit.
  let lx = max(20.0'f32, MX * 0.5'f32 - 110.0'f32) + pencilDX
  let ly = SH * 0.44'f32 + 236.0'f32 + pencilDY
  let cxp = lx + 105.0'f32
  let cyp = ly + 7.0'f32

  d = d.save().translate(cxp, cyp).rotate(-24.0'f32).translate(-cxp, -cyp)
  # Soft drop shadow under pencil
  d = d.rect(lx + 10.0'f32, ly + 4.5'f32, 198.0'f32, 11.5'f32, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28'f32), 3.0'f32)
  # Wooden cone & graphite tip (inside rotated frame, with 0xFF alpha)
  d = d.svgPath("M0 7L22 1.5V12.5Z", lx, ly, 1.0'f32, 0xFFE7C9A0'u32)
  d = d.svgPath("M0 7L7 5.2V8.8Z", lx, ly, 1.0'f32, 0xFF3B3430'u32)
  # 3-stop yellow cedar barrel (#f0c24a -> #d9a11f -> #a8770f)
  gradRectV(d, lx + 22.0'f32, ly + 1.5'f32, 160.0'f32, 11.0'f32, 0.0'f32, pencilStops, 1.0'f32, 12)
  # Hexagonal barrel facets
  d = d.line(lx + 22.0'f32, ly + 5.2'f32, lx + 182.0'f32, ly + 5.2'f32, rgba(120, 80, 10, 0.35'f32), 0.6'f32)
  d = d.line(lx + 22.0'f32, ly + 8.8'f32, lx + 182.0'f32, ly + 8.8'f32, rgba(120, 80, 10, 0.35'f32), 0.6'f32)
  # Metal ferrule & crimps
  d = d.rect(lx + 182.0'f32, ly + 1.0'f32, 12.0'f32, 12.0'f32, 0xFFC7CBCD'u32)
  d = d.line(lx + 185.0'f32, ly + 1.0'f32, lx + 185.0'f32, ly + 13.0'f32, 0xFF8F979B'u32, 0.8'f32)
  d = d.line(lx + 189.0'f32, ly + 1.0'f32, lx + 189.0'f32, ly + 13.0'f32, 0xFF8F979B'u32, 0.8'f32)
  # Pink rubber eraser
  d = d.rect(lx + 194.0'f32, ly + 1.5'f32, 15.0'f32, 11.0'f32, 0xFFE58F95'u32, 3.0'f32)
  d = d.restore()

proc drawTagAndLabelDetails(d: var Draw) =
  ## Draws the HTML pseudo-elements that Yantra's rectangular `nkPanel` cannot
  ## express natively:
  ##   1. `.label::before` and `.label::after` masking-tape strips at the top
  ##      corners of the header title card (html:90-92).
  ##   2. `#tag` luggage-tag left triangle point and dark punched eyelet hole
  ##      (`::before`, html:127).
  let lblX = 28.0'f32
  let lblY = 22.0'f32
  let lblW = 397.0'f32
  let tlCx = lblX - 14.0'f32 + 31.0'f32
  let tlCy = lblY - 10.0'f32 + 11.0'f32
  d = d.svgPath(rotQuad(tlCx, tlCy, -5.0'f32, lblX - 14.0'f32, lblY - 10.0'f32, 62.0'f32, 22.0'f32),
                0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, rgba(236, 224, 190, 0.72'f32))
  let trX = lblX + lblW - 50.0'f32
  let trCx = trX + 31.0'f32
  let trCy = lblY - 10.0'f32 + 11.0'f32
  d = d.svgPath(rotQuad(trCx, trCy, 6.0'f32, trX, lblY - 10.0'f32, 62.0'f32, 22.0'f32),
                0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, rgba(236, 224, 190, 0.72'f32))

  let tx = MX + MW + max(18.0'f32, (SW - MX - MW) * 0.5'f32 - 140.0'f32)
  let ty = MY + 175.0'f32 * U
  let th = 112.0'f32
  let nosePath = "M" & $(tx - 20.0'f32) & " " & $(ty + th * 0.5'f32) &
                 "L" & $(tx + 2.0'f32) & " " & $ty &
                 "L" & $(tx + 2.0'f32) & " " & $(ty + th) & "Z"
  d = d.svgPath(nosePath, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, 0xFFEFDFC3'u32)
  d = d.circle(tx - 4.0'f32, ty + th * 0.5'f32, 4.5'f32, cTagHole)

proc drawProps(d: var Draw) =
  drawCard(d)
  drawPencil(d)
  drawTagAndLabelDetails(d)

# ─────────────────────── 9. Marlowe 44 Typewriter Illustration Engine
const
  shN = 28
  shKnee = 0.55'f32

proc shAlpha(t: float32): float32 =
  if t <= shKnee: 0.72'f32 + (0.35'f32 - 0.72'f32) * (t / shKnee)
  else: 0.35'f32 * (1.0'f32 - t) / (1.0'f32 - shKnee)

proc drawMachineShadow(d: var Draw) =
  let sx = MX - MW * 0.06'f32
  let sy = MY + 300.0'f32 * U
  let rx = MW * 0.56'f32
  let ry = 210.0'f32 * U * 0.5'f32
  d = d.save().translate(sx + rx, sy + ry)
  for k in 0 ..< shN:
    let tOut = 1.0'f32 - k.float32 / shN.float32
    let tIn  = 1.0'f32 - (k + 1).float32 / shN.float32
    let aOut = shAlpha(tOut)
    let aIn  = shAlpha(tIn)
    let p = 1.0'f32 - (1.0'f32 - aIn) / max(1.0'f32 - aOut, 1.0'f32 / 255.0'f32)
    d = d.circle(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, ry * tOut, rgba(12, 6, 2, clampF(p, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)))
  d = d.restore()

proc drawRear(d: var Draw; left: float32) =
  ## Unfed rear portion of the sheet leaning back in perspective (html:33-42).
  let (_, _, rdy, ra) = sheetFx()
  if ra < 1.0'f32:
    d = d.save().alpha(ra)
  let baseY = TM + line.float32 * LH
  let unfed = max(0.0'f32, PH - baseY - 34.0'f32)
  let hgt = unfed * S * 0.94'f32
  let top = MY + 167.0'f32 * U - hgt + rdy
  let sheetInset = 35.0'f32 * U
  let x0 = left + sheetInset
  let ww = PW * S - sheetInset * 2.0'f32
  const REAR_N = 18
  for e in 0 ..< REAR_N:
    let f = e.float32 / REAR_N.float32
    let yAt = top + hgt * f
    let taper = 26.0'f32 * (yAt - 200.0'f32) / 125.0'f32 + 8.0'f32
    let bandCol = gradRamp(rearPaperStops, f)
    d = d.rect(x0 - taper * 0.5'f32, yAt, ww + taper, hgt / REAR_N.float32 + 1.0'f32, bandCol)
  let topTaper = 26.0'f32 * (top - 200.0'f32) / 125.0'f32 + 8.0'f32
  d = d.rect(x0 - topTaper * 0.5'f32, top, ww + topTaper, 6.0'f32 * U, 0x99FFFFFF'u32)
  if ra < 1.0'f32:
    d = d.restore()

proc drawCarriageBack(d: var Draw; shift: float32) =
  ## Rear half of the moving carriage: paper table, rubber platen roller, and rail.
  d = d.save().translate(shift, 0.0'f32)
  gradShapeV(d, P_PLATEN, cx(0.0'f32), cy(0.0'f32), U, 14.0'f32, 44.0'f32,
             cx(148.0'f32), cx(852.0'f32),
             [(0.0'f32, 0xFFB6D8C7'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFF7FAE98'u32)], 0.95'f32, 16)
  drawPlaten(d, cx(96.0'f32), cy(36.0'f32), 808.0'f32 * U, 68.0'f32 * U)
  gradRectV(d, cx(70.0'f32), cy(98.0'f32), 860.0'f32 * U, 10.0'f32 * U, 4.0'f32 * U,
            railStops, 1.0'f32, 10)
  d = d.restore()

proc drawFrontSheet(d: var Draw) =
  ## Typed front part of the sheet rising from the platen, clipped at MY+104*U.
  let x0 = carX
  d = d.save()
  d = d.clip(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, SW, MY + 104.0'f32 * U)
  let (fdy, frot, _, fa) = sheetFx()
  if fa < 1.0'f32:
    d = d.alpha(fa)
  if fdy != 0.0'f32 or frot != 0.0'f32:
    let ccx = x0 + PW * S * 0.5'f32
    let ccy = paperY + PH * S * 0.5'f32
    d = d.translate(ccx, ccy + fdy).rotate(frot).translate(-ccx, -ccy)
  d = d.layer(paperLayer, x0, paperY, PW * S, PH * S)
  # Curl shadow where paper wraps the platen
  let curlY = PRINTY - 8.0'f32 * U
  for e in 0 ..< 10:
    let f = e.float32 / 9.0'f32
    d = d.rect(x0 - 2.0'f32, curlY + 34.0'f32 * U * f, PW * S + 4.0'f32,
               34.0'f32 * U / 10.0'f32 + 1.0'f32,
               withA(0xFF281408'u32, f * f * 0.45'f32))
  d = d.restore()

proc leverAngle(): float32 =
  if leverT < 0.0'f32: return 0.0'f32
  let t = min(leverT / LEVER_PULL_MS, 1.0'f32)
  if t < 0.30'f32:
    26.0'f32 * bezier(easePull, t / 0.30'f32)
  else:
    26.0'f32 * (1.0'f32 - bezier(easePull, (t - 0.30'f32) / 0.70'f32))

proc drawLever(d: var Draw) =
  let ang = leverAngle()
  if ang != 0.0'f32:
    d = d.save().translate(cx(84.0'f32), cy(44.0'f32)).rotate(ang).translate(-cx(84.0'f32), -cy(44.0'f32))
  let armCol = if hoverKey == "@RETURN": 0xFFE6EBEE'u32 else: cChrome
  d = d.svgPath(LEVER_ARM, cx(0.0'f32), cy(0.0'f32), U, armCol, 9.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath(LEVER_ARM, cx(-1.0'f32), cy(-2.0'f32), U, 0x8CFFFFFF'u32, 2.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(18.0'f32, 7.0'f32, 14.0'f32, 10.0'f32), cx(0.0'f32), cy(0.0'f32), U, armCol)
  d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(15.0'f32, 4.0'f32, 6.0'f32, 3.0'f32), cx(0.0'f32), cy(0.0'f32), U, 0xB3FFFFFF'u32)
  d = d.circle(cx(84.0'f32), cy(44.0'f32), 9.0'f32 * U, 0xFF6F777C'u32)
  d = d.circle(cx(84.0'f32), cy(44.0'f32), 8.0'f32 * U, armCol)
  if ang != 0.0'f32:
    d = d.restore()

proc drawCarriageFront(d: var Draw; shift: float32) =
  ## Front half of carriage: paper bail, rollers, margin scale (0..48), bell marker, knobs & lever.
  d = d.save().translate(shift, 0.0'f32)
  gradRectV(d, cx(116.0'f32), cy(61.6'f32), 768.0'f32 * U, 3.2'f32 * U, 1.6'f32 * U, chromeStops, 1.0'f32, 6)
  d = d.rect(cx(216.0'f32), cy(58.6'f32), 30.0'f32 * U, 9.0'f32 * U, 0xFF1B1817'u32, 4.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.rect(cx(754.0'f32), cy(58.6'f32), 30.0'f32 * U, 9.0'f32 * U, 0xFF1B1817'u32, 4.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.line(cx(216.0'f32), cy(60.2'f32), cx(246.0'f32), cy(60.2'f32), rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18'f32), 1.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.line(cx(754.0'f32), cy(60.2'f32), cx(784.0'f32), cy(60.2'f32), rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18'f32), 1.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath(P_BAIL_L, cx(0.0'f32), cy(0.0'f32), U, cChrome, 3.4'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath(P_BAIL_R, cx(0.0'f32), cy(0.0'f32), U, cChrome, 3.4'f32 * U)

  # Margin scale bar & ticks aligned with paper columns
  let rel = S / U
  let mid = 500.0'f32 - (PW * 0.5'f32) * rel
  gradRectV(d, cx(130.0'f32), cy(89.0'f32), 740.0'f32 * U, 12.0'f32 * U, 2.0'f32 * U, chromeStops, 1.0'f32, 12)
  d = d.line(cx(130.0'f32), cy(89.6'f32), cx(870.0'f32), cy(89.6'f32), 0xCCFFFFFF'u32, 0.8'f32 * U)
  for c in 0 .. COLS:
    let x = cx(mid + (ML + c.float32 * CW + CW * 0.5'f32) * rel)
    let big = (c mod 5) == 0
    d = d.line(x, cy(90.0'f32), x, cy(90.0'f32) + (if big: 4.5'f32 else: 2.6'f32) * U,
               0xFF3B3634'u32, (if big: 1.1'f32 else: 0.7'f32) * U)
    if big:
      d = d.textCentered(x, cy(99.4'f32), 5.2'f32 * U, 0xFF2B2725'u32, "Courier New", $c, 700)

  # Red bell marker 6 columns from right margin
  let bx = cx(mid + (ML + float32(COLS - 6) * CW + CW * 0.5'f32) * rel)
  d = d.svgPath("M0 0 l2.6 -4.2 h-5.2 Z", bx, cy(94.0'f32), U, cRed)

  # Left and right knurled platen knobs + chromeH collars
  drawKnob(d, cx(44.0'f32), cy(40.0'f32), 46.0'f32 * U, 60.0'f32 * U, 10.0'f32 * U, 7)
  gradRectH(d, cx(88.0'f32), cy(56.0'f32), 10.0'f32 * U, 28.0'f32 * U, 3.0'f32 * U, chromeHStops, 1.0'f32, 6)
  drawKnob(d, cx(904.0'f32), cy(34.0'f32), 62.0'f32 * U, 72.0'f32 * U, 12.0'f32 * U, 7)
  gradRectH(d, cx(900.0'f32), cy(54.0'f32), 10.0'f32 * U, 32.0'f32 * U, 3.0'f32 * U, chromeHStops, 1.0'f32, 6)

  drawLever(d)
  d = d.restore()

proc drawBody(d: var Draw) =
  # Three-quarter right cheek
  gradShapeH(d, P_SIDE, cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 900.0'f32, 992.0'f32, 515.0'f32, 800.0'f32,
             [(0.0'f32, 0xFF7AA893'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFF4F7766'u32)])
  # Main mint-green enamel shell
  gradShapeV(d, P_SHELL, cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0.0'f32, 332.0'f32, 240.0'f32, 1060.0'f32,
             [(0.0'f32, 0xFFC3E2D2'u32), (0.18'f32, 0xFFA9D1BE'u32),
              (0.55'f32, 0xFF9FC9B6'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFF78A792'u32)], 1.0'f32, 64)
  d = d.line(cx(110.0'f32), by(6.0'f32), cx(890.0'f32), by(6.0'f32), 0x8CFFFFFF'u32, 2.0'f32 * U)
  # Top deck
  gradShapeV(d, P_DECK, cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 4.0'f32, 88.0'f32, 240.0'f32, 1060.0'f32,
             [(0.0'f32, 0xFFD3ECDF'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFFA3CDB9'u32)], 1.0'f32, 32)
  d = d.svgPath("M94 70 Q300 88 500 88 Q700 88 906 70", cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U,
                0x733C6455'u32, 2.0'f32 * U)

  # Typebasket opening + all 24 fanned typebars (html:289-292)
  radialShape(d, P_BASKET, cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U,
              cx(330.0'f32), by(4.0'f32), cx(670.0'f32), by(76.0'f32), cx(500.0'f32), by(4.0'f32),
              340.0'f32 * U, 72.0'f32 * U,
              [(0.0'f32, 0xFF050404'u32), (0.7'f32, 0xFF1C1917'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFF2D2825'u32)], 12)
  d = d.svgPath(P_TYPEBARS_L, cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xE69AA2A6'u32, 2.2'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath(P_TYPEBARS_R, cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xE69AA2A6'u32, 2.2'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath("M330 4 Q330 74 500 76 Q670 74 670 4", cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U,
                0xFFA7AFB4'u32, 4.0'f32 * U)

  # Ribbon cups & multi-stop spools (`spoolG`)
  for cxp in [220.0'f32, 780.0'f32]:
    gradShapeV(d, ellipsePath(cxp, 30.0'f32, 78.0'f32, 23.0'f32), cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U,
               7.0'f32, 53.0'f32, cx(cxp - 80.0'f32), cx(cxp + 80.0'f32), chromeStops, 1.0'f32, 14)
    d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(cxp, 29.0'f32, 66.0'f32, 17.0'f32), cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFF3B3532'u32)
    d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(cxp, 29.0'f32, 60.0'f32, 15.5'f32), cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFF231F1D'u32)
    d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(cxp, 29.0'f32, 20.0'f32, 5.2'f32), cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFF151211'u32)
    d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(cxp, 29.0'f32, 16.5'f32, 4.3'f32), cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFF8B9297'u32)
    d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(cxp, 29.0'f32, 11.0'f32, 4.0'f32), cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFFC9CED1'u32)

  # Ribbon paths (black & red bands feeding the vibrator)
  d = d.svgPath("M286 26 Q420 8 488 2", cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFF1D1A19'u32, 7.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath("M714 26 Q580 8 512 2", cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFF1D1A19'u32, 7.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath("M286 29 Q420 11 488 5", cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFF9E2019'u32, 3.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath("M714 29 Q580 11 512 5", cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 0xFF9E2019'u32, 3.0'f32 * U)

  # "Marlowe 44" front badge (centered & baseline-corrected)
  d = d.rect(cx(413.0'f32), by(89.0'f32), 174.0'f32 * U, 32.0'f32 * U, 0xFF6F777C'u32, 16.0'f32 * U)
  gradRectV(d, cx(414.0'f32), by(90.0'f32), 172.0'f32 * U, 30.0'f32 * U, 15.0'f32 * U, chromeStops, 1.0'f32, 14)
  d = d.rect(cx(420.0'f32), by(95.0'f32), 160.0'f32 * U, 20.0'f32 * U, 0xFF1E3A33'u32, 10.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.textCentered(cx(486.0'f32), by(111.0'f32), 18.5'f32 * U, 0xFFE9EEF0'u32, "Georgia", "Marlowe", 400)
  d = d.circle(cx(552.0'f32), by(105.0'f32), 12.2'f32 * U, 0xFFE9EEF0'u32)
  d = d.circle(cx(552.0'f32), by(105.0'f32), 11.0'f32 * U, cRed)
  d = d.textCentered(cx(552.0'f32), by(109.5'f32), 11.5'f32 * U, 0xFFFFF5E6'u32, "Courier New", "44", 700)

  # Keyboard well
  gradShapeV(d, P_WELL, cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, 120.0'f32, 314.0'f32, 240.0'f32, 1060.0'f32,
             [(0.0'f32, 0xFF5F8A77'u32), (0.25'f32, 0xFF83B19C'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFF8DBBA6'u32)], 0.85'f32, 48)

  # Rubber feet & left-body specular gloss curve (html:330-332)
  d = d.rect(cx(58.0'f32), by(326.0'f32), 80.0'f32 * U, 16.0'f32 * U, 0xFF1A1512'u32, 7.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.rect(cx(862.0'f32), by(326.0'f32), 80.0'f32 * U, 16.0'f32 * U, 0xFF1A1512'u32, 7.0'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath(P_GLOSS, cx(0.0'f32), by(0.0'f32), U, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14'f32), 10.0'f32 * U)

# ─────────────────────────────────────────── 10. Keyboard & Guide Point
# SINGLE key table: geometry + labels in one place. drawKeys, hitKey, keyFor
# and the legends all index KEYS, so counts cannot diverge (an IndexDefect in
# wasm is an instant trap). Every keys[i] access below is bounds-guarded.
type KeyRow = tuple[y, r, sp, dx: float32; keys: seq[string]]
let KEYS: array[4, KeyRow] = [
  (y: 150.0'f32, r: 16.5'f32, sp: 58.0'f32, dx: 10.0'f32,
   keys: @["1", "2", "3", "4", "5", "6", "7", "8", "9", "0", "-", "BS"]),
  (y: 190.0'f32, r: 17.5'f32, sp: 61.0'f32, dx:  6.0'f32,
   keys: @["TAB", "q", "w", "e", "r", "t", "y", "u", "i", "o", "p"]),
  (y: 230.0'f32, r: 18.5'f32, sp: 64.0'f32, dx:  3.0'f32,
   keys: @["LOCK", "a", "s", "d", "f", "g", "h", "j", "k", "l", ";"]),
  (y: 270.0'f32, r: 19.5'f32, sp: 67.0'f32, dx:  0.0'f32,
   keys: @["SHIFT", "z", "x", "c", "v", "b", "n", "m", ",", ".", "/", "SHIFT"])
]
const
  CAP_STOPS: array[3, GStop] = [
    (0.0'f32, 0xFFFFFCF4'u32), (0.6'f32, 0xFFF3EAD3'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFFD9CAA5'u32)
  ]
  CAPLIT_STOPS: array[3, GStop] = [
    (0.0'f32, 0xFFFFF6DC'u32), (0.6'f32, 0xFFF5D98F'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFFD2AD5C'u32)
  ]
  SPACE_STOPS: array[3, GStop] = [
    (0.0'f32, 0xFFFFFAF0'u32), (0.6'f32, 0xFFEFE4C8'u32), (1.0'f32, 0xFFCDBD96'u32)
  ]

proc shiftedOf(c: char): char =
  ## Single shift map for BOTH hardware and virtual keys (absorbs usShifted).
  case c
  of '1': '!'
  of '2': '"'
  of '3': '#'
  of '4': '$'
  of '5': '%'
  of '6': '_'
  of '7': '&'
  of '8': '\''
  of '9': '('
  of '0': ')'
  of '-': '*'
  of '=': '+'
  of '[': '{'
  of ']': '}'
  of ';': ':'
  of '\'': '"'
  of ',': '<'
  of '.': '>'
  of '/': '?'
  of char(96): '~'
  of '\\': '|'
  else: c

# Momentary hardware-shift latch: the physical Shift key feeds the SAME
# onScreenKey path as the SHIFT keycap (one action per key, no divergence).
var hwShiftOnce = false

proc setLit(k: string) =
  litKey = k
  litKeyTimer = 0.0'f32

proc scaleStops(s: array[3, GStop]; factor: int32): array[3, GStop] =
  for i in 0 .. 2:
    let c = s[i].c
    let r = clamp(int32((c shr 16) and 0xFF'u32) * factor div 100, 0'i32, 255'i32)
    let g = clamp(int32((c shr 8) and 0xFF'u32) * factor div 100, 0'i32, 255'i32)
    let b = clamp(int32(c and 0xFF'u32) * factor div 100, 0'i32, 255'i32)
    result[i] = (s[i].o, 0xFF000000'u32 or (uint32(r) shl 16) or (uint32(g) shl 8) or uint32(b))

proc keyCapFace(d: Draw; x, y, r, ry: float32; lit, hov, down: bool): Draw =
  const NC = 4
  const NR = 5
  let gx = x - 0.16'f32 * r
  let gy = y - 0.36'f32 * ry
  let cw = 2.0'f32 * r / NC.float32
  let ch = 2.0'f32 * ry / NR.float32
  let path = ellipsePath(x, y, r, ry)
  let baseStops = if lit: CAPLIT_STOPS else: CAP_STOPS
  let stops = if down: scaleStops(baseStops, 93)
              elif hov: scaleStops(baseStops, 103)
              else: baseStops
  result = d
  for ri in 0 ..< NR:
    let cyp = y - ry + ch * (float32(ri) + 0.5'f32)
    let v = (cyp - gy) / ry
    for ci in 0 ..< NC:
      let cxp = x - r + cw * (float32(ci) + 0.5'f32)
      let u = (cxp - gx) / r
      result = result.save()
      result = result.clip(x - r + cw * float32(ci) - 0.75'f32,
                           y - ry + ch * float32(ri) - 0.75'f32,
                           cw + 1.5'f32, ch + 1.5'f32)
      result = result.svgPath(path, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32,
                              gradRamp(stops, sqrt(u * u + v * v) / 1.5'f32))
      result = result.restore()

proc drawKeys(d: var Draw) =
  for ri in 0 ..< KEYS.len:
    let row = KEYS[ri]
    let keys = row.keys
    let n = keys.len
    let x0 = 500.0'f32 + row.dx - float32(n - 1) * row.sp * 0.5'f32
    for i in 0 ..< n:
      if i >= keys.len: continue
      let k = keys[i]
      let x = cx(x0 + i.float32 * row.sp)
      let y = by(row.y)
      let r = row.r
      let ry = r * 0.8'f32
      let held = (litKey == k)
      let dy = if held: 4.0'f32 * U else: 0.0'f32
      # Key stem
      d = d.rect(x - 2.6'f32 * U, y, 5.2'f32 * U, (26.0'f32 + ri.float32 * 2.0'f32) * U, 0xFF34302D'u32)
      if held:
        d = d.save().translate(x, y + dy).scale(0.965'f32, 0.965'f32).translate(-x, -y)
      # Key drop shadow, chrome rim, and radial cream cap
      d = d.svgPath(ellipsePath(x, y + 3.0'f32 * U + dy, (r + 3.5'f32) * U, (ry + 3.5'f32) * U),
                    0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32, withA(0xFF141E19'u32, 0.35'f32))
      let rimRx = (r + 3.0'f32) * U
      let rimRy = (ry + 3.0'f32) * U
      gradShapeV(d, ellipsePath(x, y + dy, rimRx, rimRy), 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32,
                 y + dy - rimRy, y + dy + rimRy, x - rimRx - 1.0'f32, x + rimRx + 1.0'f32,
                 rimStops, 1.0'f32, 8)
      let isLit = (k == "SHIFT" and shiftOn and not lockOn) or (k == "LOCK" and lockOn)
      d = keyCapFace(d, x, y - 0.6'f32 * U + dy, r * U, ry * U, isLit, k == hoverKey, held)

      # Keycap legends: centered horizontally and seated on exact SVG baseline
      let long = k.len > 1
      let label = if k == "BS": "\u232B" elif not long: $toUpperAscii(k[0]) else: k
      let fs = if long: (if k == "SHIFT": 6.6'f32 else: 7.4'f32) else: r * 0.95'f32
      let ty = if long: 2.6'f32 else: r * 0.34'f32
      d = d.textCentered(x, y + ty * U + dy, fs * U, 0xFF2B2724'u32, "Courier New", label, 700)

      if k.len == 1 and shiftedOf(k[0]) != k[0]:
        d = d.textCentered(x + r * 0.52'f32 * U, y - r * 0.22'f32 * U + dy,
                           r * 0.50'f32 * U, 0xFF8A2A22'u32, "Courier New", $shiftedOf(k[0]), 700)
      if held:
        d = d.restore()

  # Space bar
  let sDown = (litKey == " ")
  let sHov  = (hoverKey == " ")
  let sdy = if sDown: 4.0'f32 * U else: 0.0'f32
  d = d.rect(cx(300.0'f32), by(302.0'f32), 400.0'f32 * U, 9.0'f32 * U, 0xFF3D3A37'u32, 3.0'f32 * U)
  gradRectV(d, cx(292.0'f32), by(284.0'f32) + sdy, 416.0'f32 * U, 24.0'f32 * U, 12.0'f32 * U,
            rimStops, 1.0'f32, 12)
  let spStops = if sDown: scaleStops(SPACE_STOPS, 93)
                elif sHov: scaleStops(SPACE_STOPS, 103)
                else: SPACE_STOPS
  gradRectV(d, cx(298.0'f32), by(287.0'f32) + sdy, 404.0'f32 * U, 17.0'f32 * U, 8.5'f32 * U,
            spStops, 1.0'f32, 14)

proc midBell(): float32 =
  let rel = S / U
  let m = 500.0'f32 - (PW * 0.5'f32) * rel
  cx(m + (ML + float32(COLS - 6) * CW + CW * 0.5'f32) * rel)

proc drawGuide(d: var Draw; px: float32) =
  # Active typebar strike animation (invisible at rest, flashes during keystroke)
  if strikeFlash > 0.01'f32:
    d = d.line(cx(500.0'f32), cy(168.0'f32), px, cy(80.0'f32),
               withA(0xFFF3F5F6'u32, strikeFlash * 0.90'f32), 6.0'f32 * U)

  # Ribbon vibrator (rises 3.8u when red ribbon is selected)
  let vy = if red: -3.8'f32 else: 0.0'f32
  d = d.rect(px - 17.0'f32 * U, cy(81.5'f32) + vy * U, 34.0'f32 * U, 3.8'f32 * U, 0xFF1D1A19'u32)
  d = d.rect(px - 17.0'f32 * U, cy(85.3'f32) + vy * U, 34.0'f32 * U, 3.8'f32 * U, 0xFFA4221B'u32)
  # Type guide prongs & red column pointer
  d = d.svgPath("M-24 89.5 V79 Q-24 76 -21 76 M24 89.5 V79 Q24 76 21 76",
                px, cy(0.0'f32), U, 0xFFE5E9EB'u32, 2.4'f32 * U)
  d = d.line(px, cy(88.5'f32), px, cy(101.0'f32), cRed, 1.6'f32 * U)
  d = d.svgPath("M-3.4 86 H3.4 L0 90.2 Z", px, cy(0.0'f32), U, cRed)

  if strikeFlash > 0.01'f32:
    d = d.circle(px, cy(72.0'f32), (17.0'f32 - 8.0'f32 * strikeFlash) * U,
                 withA(0xFFFFF0C8'u32, strikeFlash * 0.70'f32))
  if bellFlash > 0.01'f32:
    d = d.circle(midBell(), cy(72.0'f32), 14.0'f32 * U, withA(cLamp, bellFlash * 0.35'f32))

# ─────────────────────────────────────────── 11. Lamp-Beam Dust Motes
proc seedMotes() =
  if motes.len > 0: return
  var r = rngInit(9)
  for _ in 0 ..< 46:
    motes.add Mote(
      x: r.nextF(),
      y: r.nextF(),
      z: 0.4'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.9'f32,
      s: 0.3'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.7'f32,
      p: r.nextF() * 6.28318'f32
    )

proc drawMotes(d: var Draw; tSec: float32) =
  seedMotes()
  let tMs = tSec * 1000.0'f32
  var rngSeed = uint32(tMs) and 0x7FFFFFFF'u32
  d = d.save().blend(bScreen)
  for i in 0 ..< motes.len:
    motes[i].y -= 0.0384'f32 * motes[i].s * frameSec
    motes[i].x += sin(tMs * 0.0003'f32 + motes[i].p) * 0.0048'f32 * frameSec
    if motes[i].y < -0.05'f32:
      motes[i].y = 1.05'f32
      rngSeed = rngSeed * 1664525'u32 + 1013904223'u32
      motes[i].x = float32((rngSeed shr 8) and 0x00FFFFFF'u32) / 16777216.0'f32
    let m = motes[i]
    let dx = m.x - 0.20'f32
    let dy = m.y - 0.05'f32
    let beam = max(0.0'f32, 1.0'f32 - sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * 1.35'f32)
    if beam > 0.0'f32:
      let spread = 0.18'f32 + m.y * 0.34'f32
      let fall = sqrt(max(0.0'f32, 1.0'f32 - (dx * dx) / (spread * spread)))
      let cone = 0.30'f32 + 0.70'f32 * fall * (0.45'f32 + 0.55'f32 * m.y)
      let twinkle = 0.35'f32 + 0.35'f32 * sin(tMs * 0.002'f32 + m.p * 3.0'f32)
      let a = beam * cone * twinkle
      if a > 0.01'f32:
        let x = m.x * FW
        let y = m.y * VH
        let rad = m.z * 1.3'f32
        for s in 0 ..< 4:
          let f = 1.0'f32 - s.float32 / 4.0'f32
          let rr = rad * (1.0'f32 + s.float32 * 0.55'f32)
          let sa = a * (0.10'f32 + 0.30'f32 * f * f)
          d = d.circle(x, y, rr, rgba(255, 226, 170, sa))
        if a > 0.25'f32 and rad > 0.9'f32:
          d = d.blend(eraser).circle(x, y, rad * 0.42'f32, 0xFF000000'u32).blend(bScreen)
          d = d.circle(x, y, rad * 0.40'f32, rgba(255, 236, 196, a * 0.55'f32))
  d = d.restore()

# ─────────────────────────────────────────── 12. Full Scene Composition
proc drawDingFlourish(d: var Draw; x, y, a: float32) =
  var curX = x
  for ch in "ding":
    d = d.textBaseline(curX, y, 15.0'f32, withA(0xFFFFE6AE'u32, a), "Courier New", $ch, 400)
    curX += 12.0'f32

proc buildScene(): string =
  var d = Draw()
  d = d.layer(deskLayer, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32)
  d = d.rect(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, FW, VH, 0x00000000'u32)
  drawProps(d)
  drawMachineShadow(d)

  let carShift = carX - (SW * 0.5'f32 - (PW * 0.5'f32) * S)
  drawRear(d, carX)
  drawCarriageBack(d, carShift)
  drawFrontSheet(d)
  drawCarriageFront(d, carShift)
  drawBody(d)
  drawKeys(d)
  drawGuide(d, cx((X0 - MX) / U))
  drawMotes(d, nowMs / 1000.0'f32)

  if dingT >= 0.0'f32:
    let f = min(dingT / 1300.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
    let dx = cx((X0 - MX) / U) + 40.0'f32 * U
    let da = min(1.0'f32, f / 0.15'f32) * (1.0'f32 - f)
    drawDingFlourish(d, dx, MY + 20.0'f32 * U + 6.0'f32 - f * 40.0'f32, da)

  if toastMsg.len > 0:
    let aIn = min(1.0'f32, toastT / 0.35'f32)
    let aOut = min(1.0'f32, (2.95'f32 - toastT) / 0.35'f32)
    let a = clampF(min(aIn, aOut), 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
    let tw = measureAdv(toastMsg, 13.0'f32, "Courier New", 400) + 36.0'f32
    let tx = SW * 0.5'f32 - tw * 0.5'f32
    let ty = 92.0'f32 + (1.0'f32 - aIn) * -10.0'f32
    d = d.rect(tx, ty + 6.0'f32, tw, 36.0'f32, withA(0xFF000000'u32, a * 0.25'f32), 2.0'f32)
    d = d.rect(tx, ty, tw, 36.0'f32, withA(0xFFFBF6E8'u32, a), 2.0'f32)
    d = d.textCentered(SW * 0.5'f32, ty + 23.0'f32, 13.0'f32, withA(0xFF2A2421'u32, a), "Courier New", toastMsg, 400)

  d.cmds

# ─────────────────────────── 13. Persistence, Actions & PNG Encoder
var saved      = persistent("", "marlowe44.letter.v1")
var savedMuted = persistent(0'i32, "marlowe44.muted")

proc repaint() =
  if sceneReady:
    scene.set(pDraw, buildScene())

proc toast(msg: string) =
  toastMsg = msg
  toastT = 0.0'f32

proc playS(s: Sound) =
  if not muted and s.id != 0:
    play(s)

proc persistNow() =
  var buf = ""
  let start = max(0, strokes.len - 3000)
  for i in start ..< strokes.len:
    let st = strokes[i]
    buf.add $st.ch & "," & $st.col & "," & $st.line & "," & $int(st.red) & ";"
  buf.add "|" & $sheetNo & "," & $col & "," & $line & "," & $int(red) & "," & $int(bellRung)
  saved.set(buf)

var persistPending = false

proc persistSoon() =
  ## Hang fix: never serialize + disk-write on the keystroke path. Coalesce to
  ## one write 350ms after the last key (HTML's save rhythm); line commits
  ## (Return), sheet swaps and ribbon/sound toggles still persist immediately.
  if persistPending: return
  persistPending = true
  afterMs(350.0'f32, proc() =
    persistPending = false
    persistNow())

proc checkBell() =
  if not bellRung and col >= COLS - 6:
    bellRung = true
    playS(sndBell)
    bellFlash = 1.0'f32
    dingT = 0.0'f32

proc keyFor(ch: char): string =
  if ch in {'a'..'z'}: return $ch
  if ch in {'A'..'Z'}: return $char(ord(ch) + 32)
  if ch == ' ': return " "
  for k in ['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9', '0', '-', '=', '[', ']', ';', '\'', ',', '.', '/', char(96), '\\']:
    if shiftedOf(k) == ch: return $k
  for ri in 0 ..< KEYS.len:
    if ri >= KEYS.len: continue
    for k in KEYS[ri].keys:
      if k.len == 1 and k[0] == ch: return k
  ""

proc typeChar(ch: char) =
  if line >= LINES:
    toast("The sheet is full — press New sheet for a fresh page")
    playS(sndSoft)
    return
  let c = int32(min(col, COLS))
  let s = Stroke(
    ch: ch,
    col: c,
    line: line.int32,
    red: (if red: 1'u8 else: 0'u8),
    seed: (int32(nowMs) + seedCounter * 7919) and 0x7FFFFFFF
  )
  inc seedCounter
  strokes.add s
  stampOne(s)
  setLit(keyFor(ch))
  strikeFlash = 1.0'f32
  playS(sndKey)
  if col < COLS:
    inc col
  else:
    toast("Margin locked — pull the lever or press Return")
  checkBell()
  updateCarriage(false)
  status()
  repaint()
  persistSoon()
  if shiftOn and not lockOn:
    shiftOn = false

proc doSpace() =
  setLit(" ")
  playS(sndSpace)
  if col < COLS: inc col
  checkBell()
  updateCarriage(false)
  status()
  repaint()
  persistSoon()

proc backspace() =
  setLit("BS")
  playS(sndSoft)
  if col > 0: dec col
  updateCarriage(false)
  status()
  repaint()
  persistSoon()

proc doTab() =
  setLit("TAB")
  playS(sndSpace)
  col = min(COLS, (col div 5 + 1) * 5)
  checkBell()
  updateCarriage(false)
  status()
  repaint()
  persistSoon()

proc carriageReturn() =
  playS(sndRet)
  returning = true
  returnT = 520.0'f32
  leverT = 0.0'f32
  col = 0
  bellRung = false
  if line < LINES: inc line
  if line >= LINES: toast("Last line reached — the sheet is full")
  updateCarriage(false)
  status()
  repaint()
  persistNow()

proc setRibbon(isRed: bool) =
  red = isRed
  if ribbonBtnsReady:
    bBlack.color(if red: 0x16'i32 else: 0xFF'i32, if red: 0x11'i32 else: 0xFA'i32, if red: 0x0F'i32 else: 0xF0'i32)
    bBlack.set(pTextColor, cast[int32](if red: 0xFFCFC3A8'u32 else: 0xFF2A2421'u32))
    bRed.color(if red: 0xFF'i32 else: 0x16'i32, if red: 0xFA'i32 else: 0x11'i32, if red: 0xF0'i32 else: 0x0F'i32)
    bRed.set(pTextColor, cast[int32](if red: 0xFF2A2421'u32 else: 0xFFCFC3A8'u32))
  playS(sndSoft)
  repaint()
  persistNow()

proc newSheet() =
  if sheetBusy: return
  sheetBusy = true
  sheetSwapped = false
  sheetT = 0.0'f32
  playS(sndPaper)

# Minimal uncompressed PNG encoder for Save Letter
var crcTab: array[256, uint32]
var crcReady = false

proc crcSetup() =
  if crcReady: return
  crcReady = true
  for n in 0 .. 255:
    var c = uint32(n)
    for _ in 0 ..< 8:
      c = if (c and 1'u32) != 0'u32: 0xEDB88320'u32 xor (c shr 1) else: c shr 1
    crcTab[n] = c

proc crc32(s: string): uint32 =
  crcSetup()
  var c = 0xFFFFFFFF'u32
  for ch in s:
    c = crcTab[int((c xor uint32(ord(ch))) and 0xFF'u32)] xor (c shr 8)
  c xor 0xFFFFFFFF'u32

proc adler32(s: string): uint32 =
  var a = 1'u32
  var b = 0'u32
  for ch in s:
    a = (a + uint32(ord(ch))) mod 65521'u32
    b = (b + a) mod 65521'u32
  (b shl 16) or a

proc be32(v: uint32): string =
  result = newString(4)
  result[0] = char(int((v shr 24) and 0xFF'u32))
  result[1] = char(int((v shr 16) and 0xFF'u32))
  result[2] = char(int((v shr 8) and 0xFF'u32))
  result[3] = char(int(v and 0xFF'u32))

proc pngChunk(typ, data: string): string =
  be32(uint32(data.len)) & typ & data & be32(crc32(typ & data))

proc pngLetter(w, h: int; px: seq[byte]): string =
  result = "\x89PNG\r\n\x1A\n"
  result.add pngChunk("IHDR", be32(uint32(w)) & be32(uint32(h)) & "\x08\x06\x00\x00\x00")
  # UI-thread safe: bulk row copies (no per-byte loop) so Save never hangs.
  # 1200x1552 RGBA = ~7.4MB; copyMem rows keep this to a few ms on wasm.
  var raw = newString((w * 4 + 1) * h)
  let rowBytes = w * 4
  for y in 0 ..< h:
    let dst = y * (rowBytes + 1)
    raw[dst] = '\0'
    copyMem(addr raw[dst + 1], unsafeAddr px[y * rowBytes], rowBytes)
  var zs = "\x78\x01"
  var pos = 0
  while pos < raw.len:
    let n = min(65535, raw.len - pos)
    let last = pos + n >= raw.len
    zs.add char(if last: 1 else: 0)
    zs.add char(n and 0xFF)
    zs.add char((n shr 8) and 0xFF)
    zs.add char((not n) and 0xFF)
    zs.add char(((not n) shr 8) and 0xFF)
    zs.add raw[pos ..< pos + n]
    pos += n
  zs.add be32(adler32(raw))
  result.add pngChunk("IDAT", zs)
  result.add pngChunk("IEND", "")

proc saveLetter() =
  let w = int(PW * 2.0'f32)
  let h = int(PH * 2.0'f32)
  let px = paperLayer.read(0, 0, w, h)
  if px.len != w * h * 4:
    toast("This host would not hand over the page")
    return
  let png = pngLetter(w, h, px)
  saveFile(png, "marlowe44-letter-sheet" & $sheetNo & ".png", "",
    proc(ok: bool) =
      toast(if ok: "Letter saved as a PNG — ready to post"
            else: "This host would not hand over the page"))

proc openingLines() =
  let (yr, mo, day, _) = today()
  const months = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June",
                  "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"]
  let safeMo = clamp(mo - 1, 0, 11)
  let safeYr = if yr > 0: yr else: 2026
  let safeDay = if day > 0: day else: 4
  let date = $safeDay & " " & months[safeMo] & " " & $safeYr
  var seed = 424242'i32
  proc put(txt: string; c0, l: int32) =
    for i, ch in txt:
      if ch != ' ':
        seed += 7919
        strokes.add Stroke(ch: ch, col: c0 + int32(i), line: l, red: 0'u8, seed: seed)
  put(date, int32(COLS) - int32(date.len), 0)
  put("Dear friend,", 0, 2)
  put("Forgive the smudges; the Marlowe and I are", 5, 4)
  put("still getting acquainted.", 0, 5)
  col = 26
  line = 5

proc restore(): bool =
  let raw = saved.get()
  if raw.len == 0: return false
  let parts = raw.split('|')
  if parts.len < 2: return false
  strokes = @[]
  for e in parts[0].split(';'):
    if e.len < 3: continue
    let f = e.split(',')
    if f.len < 4 or f[0].len == 0: continue
    strokes.add Stroke(
      ch: f[0][0],
      col: int32(parseInt(f[1])),
      line: int32(parseInt(f[2])),
      red: (if f[3].len > 0 and f[3][0] == '1': 1'u8 else: 0'u8),
      seed: int32(strokes.len) * 7919 + 7919
    )
  let g = parts[1].split(',')
  if g.len >= 5:
    sheetNo = int(parseInt(g[0]))
    col = parseInt(g[1])
    line = parseInt(g[2])
    red = g[3].len > 0 and g[3][0] == '1'
    bellRung = g[4].len > 0 and g[4][0] == '1'
  true

# ─────────────────────────── 14. Hit-Testing, Dragging & Frame Loop
proc hitKey(px, py: float32): string =
  # Carriage-return lever (rides with carriage in carriage space)
  let mid = SW * 0.5'f32 - (PW * 0.5'f32) * S
  let lx = cx(0.0'f32) + (carX - mid)
  if px > lx and px < lx + 100.0'f32 * U and py > cy(-8.0'f32) and py < cy(54.0'f32):
    return "@RETURN"
  if py < by(130.0'f32): return ""
  # Space bar
  if px > cx(292.0'f32) and px < cx(708.0'f32) and py > by(284.0'f32) and py < by(310.0'f32):
    return " "
  # 45 illustrated keycaps
  for ri in 0 ..< KEYS.len:
    let row = KEYS[ri]
    let keys = row.keys
    let n = keys.len
    let x0 = 500.0'f32 + row.dx - float32(n - 1) * row.sp * 0.5'f32
    for i in 0 ..< n:
      if i >= keys.len: continue
      let kx = cx(x0 + i.float32 * row.sp)
      let ky = by(row.y)
      if px > kx - row.r * U - 4.0'f32 and px < kx + row.r * U + 4.0'f32 and
         py > ky - row.r * 0.8'f32 * U - 2.0'f32 and py < ky + row.r * 0.8'f32 * U + 6.0'f32:
        return keys[i]
  ""

proc onScreenKey(k: string) =
  case k
  of "BS": backspace()
  of "TAB": doTab()
  of "SHIFT":
    shiftOn = not shiftOn
    setLit("SHIFT")
    playS(sndSoft)
    repaint()
  of "LOCK":
    lockOn = not lockOn
    shiftOn = lockOn
    playS(sndSoft)
    repaint()
  of " ": doSpace()
  of "@RETURN": carriageReturn()
  else:
    if k.len == 0: return
    let up = shiftOn or lockOn or hwShiftOnce
    hwShiftOnce = false
    var ch = k[0]
    if ch in {'a'..'z'} and up: ch = char(ord(ch) - 32)
    elif up: ch = shiftedOf(ch)
    typeChar(ch)

proc hitProp(px, py: float32): string =
  let clx = max(24.0'f32, MX * 0.5'f32 - 140.0'f32) + cardDX
  let cly = SH * 0.44'f32 + cardDY
  if px > clx - 9.0'f32 and px < clx + 271.0'f32 and
     py > cly - 12.0'f32 and py < cly + 188.0'f32:
    return "card"
  let plx = max(20.0'f32, MX * 0.5'f32 - 110.0'f32) + pencilDX
  let ply = SH * 0.44'f32 + 236.0'f32 + pencilDY
  if px > plx + 3.0'f32 and px < plx + 207.0'f32 and
     py > ply - 46.0'f32 and py < ply + 60.0'f32:
    return "pencil"
  ""

proc propDown(px, py: float32): bool =
  let p = hitProp(px, py)
  if p.len == 0: return false
  dragProp = p
  downPX = px
  downPY = py
  propMoved = false
  if p == "pencil":
    dragOX = pencilDX
    dragOY = pencilDY
  else:
    dragOX = cardDX
    dragOY = cardDY
  true

proc propMove(px, py: float32) =
  if dragProp.len == 0: return
  let dx = px - downPX
  let dy = py - downPY
  if abs(dx) + abs(dy) > 4.0'f32: propMoved = true
  if not propMoved: return
  let nx = clampF(dragOX + dx, -220.0'f32, 420.0'f32)
  let ny = clampF(dragOY + dy, -160.0'f32, 200.0'f32)
  if dragProp == "pencil":
    pencilDX = nx
    pencilDY = ny
  else:
    cardDX = nx
    cardDY = ny
  repaint()

proc propUp() =
  if dragProp.len == 0: return
  if not propMoved:
    if dragProp == "pencil":
      toast("A pencil for the margins — the Marlowe does the letters")
    else:
      toast("Operator's card — Return starts a new line")
    repaint()
  dragProp = ""

proc tick() =
  let dtSec = clampF(frameDt(), 0.0'f32, 0.10'f32)
  let dtMs = dtSec * 1000.0'f32
  nowMs += dtMs
  frameSec = dtSec

  carX = carAnim.advance(dtMs)
  paperY = paperAnim.advance(dtMs)
  if returning:
    returnT -= dtMs
    if returnT <= 0.0'f32: returning = false

  if sheetT >= 0.0'f32:
    sheetT += dtMs
    if sheetT >= 760.0'f32 and not sheetSwapped:
      sheetSwapped = true
      inc sheetNo
      strokes = @[]
      col = 0
      line = 0
      bellRung = false
      redrawAll()
      updateCarriage(true)
      status()
      persistNow()
      toast("Sheet No. " & $sheetNo & " is in the machine")
    if sheetT >= 1480.0'f32:
      sheetT = -1.0'f32
      sheetBusy = false
      sheetSwapped = false

  strikeFlash = max(0.0'f32, strikeFlash - dtSec * 6.3'f32)
  bellFlash   = max(0.0'f32, bellFlash - dtSec * 1.4'f32)
  if dingT >= 0.0'f32:
    dingT += dtMs
    if dingT > 1400.0'f32: dingT = -1.0'f32
  if toastMsg.len > 0:
    toastT += dtSec
    if toastT > 2.95'f32: toastMsg = ""
  if litKey.len > 0:
    litKeyTimer += dtSec
    if litKeyTimer > 0.11'f32: litKey = ""
  if leverT >= 0.0'f32:
    leverT += dtMs
    if leverT >= LEVER_PULL_MS: leverT = -1.0'f32
  repaint()

# ─────────────────────────────────────────── 15. Main UI Tree & Entry Point
proc main() =
  deskLayer  = newLayer(int(FW), int(VH))
  paperLayer = newLayer(int(PW * 2.0'f32), int(PH * 2.0'f32))

  sndBell  = sound("bell.wav")
  sndKey   = sound("key.wav")
  sndPaper = sound("paper.wav")
  sndRet   = sound("return.wav")
  sndSoft  = sound("soft.wav")
  sndSpace = sound("space.wav")

  layout()
  muted = savedMuted.get() == 1
  if not restore():
    openingLines()
  redrawAll()
  status()
  updateCarriage(true)
  paintDesk()
  seedMotes()

  let page = window("Carriage Return", int32(FW), int32(VH))
  page.color(0x3A, 0x22, 0x12)
  page.spacing(0)
  page.padding(0)

  let stage = stack(page)
  stage.padding(0)

  # Full-viewport drawing canvas
  scene = canvas(stage, int32(FW), int32(VH))
  sceneReady = true
  scene.set(pDraw, buildScene())

  scene.addHandler(evPointerDown, proc() =
    let (px, py) = pointerPos()
    let k = hitKey(px, py)
    if k.len > 0:
      onScreenKey(k)
      handled()
    elif propDown(px, py):
      handled())

  scene.addHandler(evPointerMove, proc() =
    let (px, py) = pointerPos()
    if dragProp.len > 0:
      propMove(px, py)
      handled()
    else:
      let h = hitKey(px, py)
      let overProp = if h.len == 0: hitProp(px, py) else: ""
      let wantCursor = if h.len > 0 or overProp.len > 0: 1'i32 else: 0'i32
      if wantCursor != lastCursor:
        lastCursor = wantCursor
        scene.set(pCursor, wantCursor)
      if h != hoverKey:
        hoverKey = h
        repaint())

  scene.addHandler(evPointerUp, proc() =
    if dragProp.len > 0:
      propUp()
      handled())

  scene.addHandler(evFrame, proc() = tick())

  # Top-left header badge (.label, html:89-94)
  let label = panel(stage)
  label.set(pInsetLeft, 28.0'f32)
  label.set(pInsetTop, 22.0'f32)
  label.width(397)
  label.padding(14, 22, 12, 22)
  label.setFill(linear(0xFBF6E8, 0xF1E8D2, 90.0))
  label.setShadow(shadow(0.0, 12.0, 24.0, style.rgba(0x000000, 0.55)))
  label.border(1, 0xE8, 0xDF, 0xC6)
  label.radius(2)

  let h1 = text(label, "Carriage Return")
  h1.fontSize(27)
  h1.set(pFontWeight, 700'i32)
  h1.color(0x2A, 0x24, 0x21)

  let sub = text(label, "a typewriter for letters — the Marlowe 44")
  sub.fontSize(13)
  sub.color(0xB3, 0x26, 0x1E)

  # Top-right header controls (.controls, html:95-113)
  let ctl = row(stage)
  ctl.set(pInsetRight, 28.0'f32)
  ctl.set(pInsetTop, 22.0'f32)
  ctl.spacing(10)
  ctl.set(pAlignItems, 1)

  proc mkBtn(parent: Node; cap: string; w: int32): Node =
    result = button(parent, cap)
    result.width(w)
    result.height(40)
    result.radius(20)
    result.setFill(linear(0xFFFAF0, 0xDCCFAE, 90.0))
    result.setShadow(shadow(0.0, 6.0, 14.0, style.rgba(0x000000, 0.50)))
    result.border(2, 0x7D, 0x86, 0x8B)
    result.fontSize(12)
    result.set(pFontWeight, 700'i32)
    result.set(pCursor, 1'i32)
    result.set(pTextColor, cast[int32](0xFF2A2421'u32))

  let seg = panel(ctl)
  seg.padding(4, 4, 4, 4)
  seg.radius(20)
  seg.setFill(linear(0x2B2320, 0x16110F, 90.0))
  seg.setShadow(shadow(0.0, 6.0, 14.0, style.rgba(0x000000, 0.50)))
  seg.border(1, 0x7D, 0x86, 0x8B)

  let segRow = row(seg)
  segRow.spacing(4)
  segRow.set(pAlignItems, 1)

  let segLbl = text(segRow, "  RIBBON ")
  segLbl.fontSize(11)
  segLbl.color(0xD9, 0xCD, 0xB2)

  proc mkSegBtn(parent: Node; cap: string; w: int32): Node =
    result = button(parent, cap)
    result.width(w)
    result.height(32)
    result.radius(16)
    result.color(0x16, 0x11, 0x0F)
    result.fontSize(12)
    result.set(pFontWeight, 700'i32)
    result.set(pCursor, 1'i32)
    result.set(pTextColor, cast[int32](0xFFCFC3A8'u32))

  bBlack = mkSegBtn(segRow, "Black", 78)
  bBlack.onClick(proc() = setRibbon(false))
  bRed = mkSegBtn(segRow, "Red", 64)
  bRed.onClick(proc() = setRibbon(true))
  ribbonBtnsReady = true
  setRibbon(red)

  let bSound = mkBtn(ctl, if muted: "Muted" else: "Sound", 98)
  bSound.onClick(proc() =
    muted = not muted
    savedMuted.set(if muted: 1'i32 else: 0'i32)
    bSound.setText(if muted: "Muted" else: "Sound"))

  let bNew = mkBtn(ctl, "New sheet", 132)
  bNew.onClick(proc() = newSheet())

  let bSave = mkBtn(ctl, "Save letter", 148)
  bSave.onClick(proc() = saveLetter())

  # Right-hand shipping tag (#tag, html:126-130, 189-194)
  let tag = panel(stage)
  tag.set(pInsetLeft, MX + MW + max(18.0'f32, (SW - MX - MW) * 0.5'f32 - 140.0'f32))
  tag.set(pInsetTop, MY + 175.0'f32 * U)
  tag.width(230)
  tag.padding(14, 16, 14, 36)
  tag.radius(2)
  tag.setFill(linear(0xF6ECD2, 0xE9DCBC, 90.0))
  tag.setShadow(shadow(0.0, 12.0, 24.0, style.rgba(0x000000, 0.45)))
  tag.border(1, 0xE0, 0xD0, 0xB0)

  let tagCol = column(tag)
  tagCol.spacing(0)

  for i, r in [("Sheet", statusSheet), ("Line", statusLine),
               ("Column", statusCol), ("Keystrokes", statusCount)]:
    let rowN = row(tagCol)
    rowN.height(21)
    rowN.set(pJustify, 3'i32)      # justify-content: space-between
    rowN.set(pAlignItems, 1'i32)   # align-items: center
    let k = text(rowN, r[0])
    k.fontSize(12)
    k.color(0x3A, 0x30, 0x29)
    k.flexGrow(1.0'f32)            # expands to push the value flush right
    let v = text(rowN, r[1])
    case i
    of 0: tagSheet = v
    of 1: tagLine  = v
    of 2: tagColN  = v
    else: tagCount = v
    v.fontSize(12)
    v.set(pFontWeight, 700'i32)
    v.color(0xB3, 0x26, 0x1E)
    v.align(alRight)

  tagReady = true

  # Root keyboard handler: hardware keys feed the SAME onScreenKey path as
  # the virtual keycaps, so every physical key produces exactly one action.
  # (ancestor key dispatch starts at focus and climbs to page)
  page.addHandler(evKey, proc() =
    let k = lastKey()
    if k.ctrl or k.alt: return
    if k == keyEnter: onScreenKey("@RETURN"); handled(); return
    if k == keyBackspace: onScreenKey("BS"); handled(); return
    if k == keyTab: onScreenKey("TAB"); handled(); return
    if k == keySpace: onScreenKey(" "); handled(); return
    if k.code >= 32 and k.code < 127:
      hwShiftOnce = k.shift
      onScreenKey($char(k.code))
      hwShiftOnce = false
      handled())

proc yantra_main*() {.exportc: "yantra_main".} =
  main()

FILE: apps/typewriter/TASK.md

# TASK: Carriage Return — A Typewriter for Letters (Marlowe 44)

## Objective
Port `029-typewriter-letters.html` and `029-typewriter-letters.txt` into a complete, 1:1 running Yantra native app in `apps/typewriter/app.nim`, compile it to `apps/typewriter/app.wasm` using `bash tools/build-wasm.sh apps/typewriter/app.nim apps/typewriter/app.wasm`, and verify with the native Yantra runner `render/native/build/desktop/yantra_run.exe`.

## Key Specifications & Requirements
1. **Visual fidelity & 1:1 recreation**:
   - Marlowe 44 typewriter in mint-green enamel (#9fc9b6), darker 3/4 cheek, top deck with ribbon spools, chrome details, script badge "Marlowe 44".
   - Procedural walnut desk with grain lines, knots, seams, warm lamplight radial gradient, bloom, and dried coffee ring.
   - Floating dust motes animated in the light beam.
   - Paper bail, platen roller, margin scale (0-48 with ticks and numbers), red bell marker at col 42, red column pointer.
   - Carriage assembly moves horizontally as typing advances.
   - Return lever swings on carriage return; carriage slides back smoothly with line feed.
   - Dual-color ribbon vibrator (black / red).
   - Side props: Operator instruction card, yellow pencil with eraser, shipping tag tracking sheet number, line, column, keystrokes.
   - Header controls: "Carriage Return" label badge, ribbon toggle (Black/Red), sound toggle, New Sheet, Save Letter.
   - Toast notifications for margin lock, sheet full, new sheet.

2. **Typing & Mechanics**:
   - Physical keyboard typing via `onKey:` on app root: printable characters, Space, Backspace (overstrikes without erasing), Tab (jumps to next multiple of 5), Return (swings lever, slides carriage, line feed).
   - On-screen clickable keys for all 4 rows + spacebar + carriage return lever.
   - Shift and Shift Lock with visual indicators and shifted characters.
   - Typewriter character stamping with realistic imperfections: baseline jitter, rotation jitter, ink density variations, ribbon weave knockout, faint double strikes.
   - Margin bell rings 6 columns before margin (col 42) with floating "ding" effect.
   - Margin lock at column 48.
   - New sheet animation and reset.
   - Initial pre-typed letter with date and salutation.
   - Session persistence for strokes, position, and sheet count.

3. **Build & Verification**:
   - Target binary: `apps/typewriter/app.wasm`
   - Build command: `bash tools/build-wasm.sh apps/typewriter/app.nim apps/typewriter/app.wasm`
   - Runner: `render/native/build/desktop/yantra_run.exe`
